© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 18

The Philippines
Energy dependence and
governance risks skew
outcomes toward the downside
The Philippines has a wide downside-skewed fan.
In its favor, attractive demographics, steady
remittance inflows, and a consumption-led model
insulate it from global trade disruption.
However, its growth architecture is vulnerable.
Dependence on imported energy quickly turns
external price shocks into household inflation, while
weak policy implementation hinders the conversion

of investment commitment into actual deployment.
Additionally, AI-driven automation creates longer-
term risk to the business process outsourcing
(BPO) sector. Without stronger energy security and
governance consistency, growth could be capped
below the Philippines’ potential.
Singapore is bound by energy dependence and
demographic constraints. To sustain the base
scenario, it will need to attract talent and make
continued productivity gains through AI and

automation. While Singapore’s baseline growth
projection is moderate, its track record suggests its
ceiling may have room to rise.
Singapore
Safe-haven depth anchors the
floor, while hub status drives
the upside
Singapore’s trajectory is a picture of structural
resilience: A contained downside is matched with
meaningful upside potential.
The country’s safe-haven status means capital and
talent will flow toward Singapore in periods of stress
rather than away, giving it a well-defended floor. In

upside scenarios, Singapore’s status as a trusted
hub translates into deeper regional intermediation,
expanded AI and advanced manufacturing activity,
and broader capital flows.
18
© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 19

Thailand
Structural headwinds compress
the range in every scenario
Thailand’s fan is the most compressed across the
region, reflecting structural constraints that are
equally binding in both directions. Its trajectory is
also shaped more by domestic constraints than by
the external environment.
The growth floor has genuine support that holds
across most conditions: an established EV,
electronics, and automotive manufacturing base;
broad trade and capital ties; and strong tourism. Yet

domestic challenges restrict growth. Demographic
decline, high household debt, and political
fragmentation suppress the upside ceiling. These
domestic constraints are unlikely to ease in any
scenario. Furthermore, balancing alignment between
Chinese supply chains and Western market access
remains an ongoing challenge.
Vietnam
The region’s highest upside
story is supported by FDI
attractiveness and its capacity to
address constraints
Vietnam has the most upside-heavy fan across the

SEA-6. Decades of reform have embedded it in the
global electronics and semiconductor supply chains,
establishing a baseline floor that cannot be quickly
undone, and the country’s broad trade relationships
could cushion the impact if exports became more
challenging.
While its floor is strong, Vietnam’s ceiling sets it
apart. Unlike other SEA-6 economies, Vietnam
captures FDI from both US- and Chinese-aligned
supply chains. Under optimal conditions, this

allows higher-value investments to scale across
logistics, industrial parks, and upstream electronics,
meaningfully compounding the base scenario.
Vietnam’s constraints—power grid disruptions and
shallow domestic capital markets—are solvable
infrastructure and financial development challenges
rather than structural deficits. Both are within
Vietnam’s capacity to address over the next decade.
19
© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 20

Priorities to Accelerate Growth
While these levers remain unchanged, the current
global climate has elevated three specific priorities:
strengthening institutional resilience, fortifying
energy systems, and capturing the AI dividend.
Our 2024 report identified
operational levers for building
durable economic advantages.
01
Strengthening Institutional
Resilience
03
Capturing the AI Dividend
02
Fortifying Energy Systems
Institutions set the foundation for investor

confidence, capital retention, and a government’s
capacity for consistent policy execution. In a volatile
and fragmented global economy, institutional
credibility is a major differentiator. Achieving it
requires progress across three dimensions:
• Governance and the rule of law anchors investor
confidence and reduces the cost of doing
business.
• Fiscal stability absorbs external shocks and
funds productive long-term investments.
• Capital market depth efficiently mobilizes

domestic savings and foreign capital into growth
opportunities.
Improvement across these three areas is self-
reinforcing: Institutional credibility deepens
domestic capital markets and attracts investments,
which fund the infrastructure and innovation needed
to sustain growth. The reverse is equally true:
Institutional erosion quickly undermines progress on
every other priority.
AI is emerging as the primary productivity lever
of the decade, offering the potential to reshape

industries and offset labor constraints. But value
capture is not guaranteed. Economic returns
will only accrue in countries that move beyond
initial adoption and surface-level integration to
deploy AI at enterprise-scale. To benefit from AI,
countries must build a talent pipeline and establish
policy conditions that make enterprise adoption
commercially viable now.
Capturing the dividend requires action across three
interconnected areas:
• Policy and infrastructure: Sovereign AI

strategies, data governance frameworks, and
domestically governed compute capacity will set
the foundation for scaled deployment.
• Enterprise adoption: This means scaling
beyond pilots into full operational integration
and embedding AI into core business processes
with measurable productivity outcomes. Tools
also need to be accessible to benefit the broader
economy, not just large firms.
• Workforce skills and development: Workforce
readiness requires building AI capabilities in

education systems while retraining the existing
workforce for AI’s labor impacts.
Energy has transformed from a basic utility into a
binding growth constraint for many SEA-6 countries.
Grid capacity and reliability determine whether an
economy can expand its industrial base and support
power-intensive AI infrastructure. Energy security
is also a lever for managing cost and security risks
as energy supply chains become more volatile. That
makes energy infrastructure a strategic priority

rather than a regulatory challenge. Fortifying energy
systems calls for:
• Reliability: Achieving a consistent, uninterrupted
power supply is the most immediate priority. This
requires rapid grid modernization, expanded
storage capacity, and secure dispatchable
generation capable of absorbing a variety of
renewables.
• Sustainability: Renewable energy assets must
become bankable through reformed procurement
frameworks, payment certainty, and credible

carbon commitments. These aren’t simply climate
gestures; they are prerequisites for attracting
high-quality investments.
• Security: Import sources must be diversified
to mitigate exposure to external supply shocks.
Countries need to build strategic reserves while
accelerating domestic baseload alternatives.
20
© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 21

The Future Will Reflect
Near-Term Policy Choices
But the sizable variance between regional growth
floors and ceilings confirms that Southeast Asia’s
trajectory depends more on domestic policy
decisions than external forces.
Institutional resilience, fortified energy systems, and
AI integration are universal regional priorities, even
though their relative importance varies based on
each country’s structural position.
Ongoing shifts in geo-economics,

technology, and domestic policy
trends make the current juncture a
sensitive one.
Indonesia
The defining test is institutional credibility.
Without governance credibility and fiscal discipline,
Indonesia will not scale to meet the energy or
AI opportunity. Both require stronger investor
confidence and disciplined execution.
Singapore
The priority is diffusion. Singapore’s platform
is exceptionally strong. Energy diversification is
underway, institutional strengths are intact, and the

country has the most advanced AI ecosystem. The
priority now is converting AI leadership into broad
productivity gains across mid-market firms and non-
technology workers to offset the tightening labor
supply.
Thailand
The critical variable is restoring institutional
momentum. Structural reforms are needed
to overcome declining growth and political
fragmentation. In addition, because EV transition
and Chinese competition are reshaping Thailand’s
core manufacturing landscape, quickly embedding

AI and advanced manufacturing capabilities into the
industrial base is a critical near-term move.
Vietnam
The primary constraint is execution. Power grid
limitations and infrastructure bottlenecks threaten
to cap the next phase of growth. To maintain its
outperformer trajectory, the near-term priority
is twofold: resolving energy reliability shortfalls
that constrain high-value manufacturing and
implementing strategic AI-led value chain upgrades
before its low-cost assembly advantage erodes.

Malaysia
The key question is conversion. Malaysia’s
strengths in AI infrastructure, energy systems, and
institutional fundamentals give the country a distinct
head start. The next phase depends on converting
this foundation into deep ecosystem value through
talent development, enterprise-level AI adoption,
and productivity gains. In turn, this also requires
policy continuity and consistent execution through
the next electoral cycle.
The Philippines

The challenge is transition. Progress on energy
and institutional resilience will enable the AI
transition, but the more immediate priority is
upgrading the BPO sector to mitigate automation
pressures. The next few years will be critical; the
Philippines must move into higher-value services
while sustaining reform momentum through its next
leadership transition.
21
© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 22

Conclusion
Southeast Asia is growing,
with considerable structural divergence.
Countries with diversified industrial bases, a thriving
services economy, stable institutional foundations,
and strong fiscal management are more likely to
weather downside risks and capture upside growth in
a recovery. Conversely, those with narrower growth
models or more constrained policy frameworks are
likely to struggle in any scenario.
In the decade ahead, countries must continuously

demonstrate institutional credibility, enhance energy
security, and absorb advanced technological
capabilities. Nations that build and fortify these
foundations will find the region’s long-term tailwinds
working in their favor.
The forecasts detailed in this report are entirely
achievable. Performance over the next two to three
years will decide which nations live up to their full
potential.
22
© From Tailwinds to Trade-Offs | Southeast Asia Outlook 2026–2035

### Page 23

Methodology
We also used selected benchmark indicators,
including the Herfindahl-Hirschman Market
Concentration Index, the World Bank’s Ease of Doing
Business score, Human Capital Index, Logistics
Performance Index, and Worldwide Governance
Indicators. For our analysis of new growth drivers,
we drew on the Global Innovation Index from the
World Intellectual Property Organization; private
technology investment and venture capital data from
PitchBook and Tracxn; capital markets data from

DBS; and energy, reliability, and emissions data from
Wood Mackenzie and the Emissions Database for
Global Atmospheric Research.
Please contact the authors for details regarding our
methodology, forecasts, or analysis.
In this report, “Southeast Asia” and the “SEA-6” refer
to Indonesia, Malaysia, the Philippines, Singapore,
Thailand, and Vietnam. While not exhaustive, these
six countries contribute to more than 95% of the
region’s GDP.

Our growth framework assessments drew from

## Document 304: Micron turning from margin to capacity expansion mode, like Nvidia - beat and rest

Source PDF: all report pdf/Micron turning from margin to capacity expansion mode, like Nvidia - beat and rest.pdf
Report source: Substack Reports
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Micron turning from margin to capacity expansion mode, like Nvidia - beat and rest
Date:    8 hours ago
Micron turning from margin to capacity expansion mode, like Nvidia - beat and rest
View in browser
Micron turning from margin to
capacity expansion mode, like
Nvidia - beat and rest
就像過去⼀年，不管  Nvidia 財務數字多美好，短期股價也不⾒激
情，我們覺得美光將步⼊  Nvidia 後塵
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS

SEP 30 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary
For a near term, we see the strong data center AI
GPU/TPU/ASIC/CPU capex and deployment to support HBM, DDR5,
NAND ﬂash demand. However, starting 4Q26 and beyond, we have
started to see the pricing hike was/is decelerating and gross margin
is topping but capex growth is accelerating. Despite months of
inventory MOI and capital density are still at a healthy level, we
believe Micron is turning itself from gross margin expansion to

capacity expansion mode. Like Nvidia in the past 12 months, no
matter how good the ﬁnancial numbers are ahead of analysts’
estimates, Micron might continue to beat the market on numbers
but rest (consolidate up/down) on share price performance in line
with the change of Sox index.
短期來看，數據算⼒中⼼領域對 AI GPU 、 TPU 、 ASIC 及 CPU 的強勁資本⽀出
與部署，仍有⼒地⽀撐著 HBM 、 DDR5 及 NAND 快閃記憶體的需求。然⽽，展
望 2026 年第四季及以後，我們觀察到價格上漲勢頭正在放緩，⽑利率已觸及
峰值，⽽資本⽀出增速卻在加快。儘管庫存⽔位（以季度庫存量 /MOI 衡量）及

資本密度指標（資本開⽀除以營收）依然健康，但我們認為美光（ Micron ）正
從「⽑利率擴張」模式轉向「產能擴張」模式。正如過去 12 個⽉的英偉達
（ Nvidia ）那樣，即便美光的財務業績持續超出分析師預期，其股價表現也可
能出現盤整（或隨 SOX 指數波動），不再單純因業績超預期⽽⼤幅上漲。

### Page 2

Key highlights
Pricing hike continues but was/is decelerating: Micron
reports 3Q26 sales of US$54.23bn, up 31% qoq and 379% yoy,
which was 7% above Bloomberg consensus estimates of 24%
qoq. Micron guides 4Q26 sales of US$60-63bn, up 11%-16%
qoq (13% qoq as mid point) and 351% yoy, which is 9% above
Bloomberg consensus estimates. We believe the 3Q26 sales
beat was largely driven by stronger than expected NAND price
hike of 30% qoq (20% qoq by Bloomberg estimates) and 394%

yoy and also 10% bit output qoq growth for NAND (5% qoq by
Bloomberg estimates);
Source: Bloomberg estimates

Cyclical gross margin expansion seems topping at 85-
87%: Despite DRAM bit price hike of 17-19% qoq and NAND bit

price hike of 30% qoq, Micron reports 3Q26 gross margin of
86.8%, up only 2ppts from 2Q26’s 84.6% but guides 4Q26
gross margin contraction to 85.95%, which is lower than
Bloomberg’s 86.7% estimates. 3Q26 operating of 80.7% and
4Q26 operating guidance of 82% are all slightly lower than

Data center HBM and NAND (KV cache oﬄoad)
customers were the key driver: Despite 3Q26 core data
center business unit sales only accounted for 33% of sales
(28% of sales in 2Q26), the business sales increased 56% qoq

Source: Micron
Healthy months of inventory: Micron reports 4.33 months of
inventory, up 8% and 8% yoy;
Healthy capital density: Micron raised 3Q26 capex 42% qoq
and 96% yoy to US$11.1bn to drive up the capital density to
16% in 3Q26 from 2Q26’s 13% but still much lower than
3Q25’s 30%. This suggests the company’s capex boost still
remaining at a healthy level;
Expanding Strategic Customer Agreements SCA: In

addition, Micron signed 26 strategic customer agreements

### Page 5

(raised from 16 in 2Q26) covering 35% of sales throughout
2030 (vs. 20% of DRAM and 33% of NAND volume in 2Q26),
with ﬂoor prices ensuring robust gross margins;
Risks for us to monitor: Till now, we believe Micron and
other major memory vendors are managing capex, supply, and
inventory well below demand extremely well. However, we will
be watching closely on how much inventories had been piled
up at the module makers and customers due to concerning

pricing hike. In addition, Micron and other memory makers
have been pushing its DRAM/NAND price to a painful level for
customers and pushing its gross margin at historical high level
of 87% for 3Q26. We thus see limited upside on ASP to boost
sales and limited upside on gross margin to boost EPS for the
next few quarters. In addition, by raising capex to boost bit
growth and sales growth might result in depreciation cost
increase to aﬀect the gross margin improvement.

## Document 305: Monitoring Bulgaria_ From euro adoption to the fiscal test

Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-01

### Page 1

Bulgaria has carried solid domestic momentum into the second
half of 2026, even as elevated inﬂation, a wider ﬁscal deﬁcit
and a weak external position complicate the post-euro picture.
The next phase of convergence will depend less on the
currency changeover and more on ﬁscal credibility, investment
execution and productivity

Stefan Posea and Valentin Tataru
Soﬁa in Bulgaria, which saw economic expansion of 2.8% in the ﬁrst half of
2026
Bulgaria's economy at a glance
GDP: Consumption and investment continue to power growth,
while net exports are keeping a foot on the brake. We forecast
growth of 2.9% in 2026, followed by 2.3% in 2027.
Industry and construction: Labour shortages and economic
uncertainty remain key constraints on industry, while
residential construction continues to expand.

slowing to 3.2% in 2027. Second-round eﬀects from the

### Page 2

sizeable ﬁscal impulse and a potentially prolonged energy
shock are the main upside risks, especially to our 2027
forecast.
Fiscal policy: Low public debt remains an important buﬀer, but
expenditure rigidities make the planned consolidation in 2027-
28 demanding.
GDP: still resilient, but increasingly
unbalanced
The economy expanded by 2.8% year-on-year in the ﬁrst half of
2026. Domestic demand remained strong, supported by a 7.1%
increase in private consumption and a 7.3% rise in investment.

However, much of this demand spilled into imports rather than
domestic production. Exports fell by 4.1%, while imports increased
by a striking 8.5%, leaving net exports to oﬀset most of the gains
from domestic demand. The picture is largely similar to 2025, but
the imbalances have become more pronounced.
In the near term, household spending should continue to beneﬁt
from a tight labour market and still-strong, double-digit wage
growth. Public investment should also advance, supported by the

ﬁnal phase of the Recovery and Resilience Facility (RRF). Whether
this public-sector push can generate a stronger and more durable
private-investment cycle will be an important factor to watch.
This is not a cost-free expansion, however. Strong demand is
keeping services inﬂation elevated, credit growth continues to
accelerate, and the current account deﬁcit is likely to remain under
pressure. We expect growth to moderate from 3.1% in 2025 to
around 2.9% in 2026 and 2.3% in 2027. The main variables to

watch are household demand, energy prices, private investment
and the pace of export normalisation.
At its core, Bulgaria remains a domestic-demand-led growth story,
but the quality and sustainability of that growth are coming under
increasing scrutiny. Euro area membership provides a stronger
institutional anchor, but risks continue to stem from the country’s
procyclical ﬁscal stance, particularly if weaker growth eventually
coincides with the need for more substantial ﬁscal adjustment.

### Page 3

One important development for both consumption and public
ﬁnances is the revised minimum wage framework. The new
approach is intended to weaken the automatic link between
average wages and the minimum wage, moderating future
increases in the wage ﬂoor. This should help contain ﬁscal costs
and wage-related inﬂation pressures from 2027 onwards.
Bulgaria’s broader credit fundamentals nevertheless remain
supportive. Public debt is still low, while euro-area membership

strengthens the country’s ﬁnancial and institutional position. These
buﬀers should help cushion the impact of rising debt and spending
pressures.
Euro adoption has also strengthened Bulgaria’s monetary and
ﬁnancial anchor. At the same time, the alignment of bank reserve
requirements with euro-area rules released liquidity previously held
for prudential purposes under the lev exchange rate regime,
improving funding conditions across the economy. While this

should support investment and ﬁnancial deepening, it also raises
the importance of macroprudential policy as credit growth and
property prices accelerate.
Industry and construction: a two-speed
investment picture
The industrial picture remains uncertain. Output fell by 4.5% year-
on-year in the ﬁrst seven months of 2026, extending the weakness
seen in 2025, when production declined by 7.2%. Manufacturing,
mining and energy output have all remained under pressure.

Weak external demand, particularly from Europe’s core
manufacturing markets, continues to weigh on export-oriented
producers. Labour shortages and uncertainty about the economic
outlook also remain among the main constraints reported by
companies.

(manufacturing)

Source: NSI, Ifo Institute, ING
Construction provides a clearer positive signal, although the
underlying picture is uneven. Between January and July, building
construction increased by 11.2% year-on-year, compared with
growth of 4.0% in civil engineering and just 0.7% in specialised
construction activities.
The strength of residential construction reﬂects a large pipeline of
projects already moving into execution. Although activity has
eased from the exceptionally strong levels recorded in early 2026,

both permits and construction starts remain well above year-
earlier levels. Development continues to be concentrated in Soﬁa,
Plovdiv, Varna and Burgas, supported by robust mortgage lending
and rapid house-price growth.
Civil engineering, by contrast, remains more dependent on public
procurement, EU reimbursements and the sequencing of major
transport and utility projects. Its slowdown is therefore more likely
to reﬂect delays and uneven project execution.

Overall, the construction sector has undergone a rotation. Private
building activity and the existing property pipeline are proving
much stronger than the execution of large infrastructure projects.

Inﬂation: Services price pressures continue
to cause trouble
Headline CPI inﬂation accelerated to 5.1% in August and averaged
around 5.0% in the ﬁrst eight months of 2026. Transport inﬂation
has stood out, remaining in double digits for much of the year,
while services inﬂation has been persistently sticky. Food inﬂation
has provided some relief, but only recently.
The main drivers have been strong domestic demand, higher
energy prices and a tight labour market that continues to push up

labour costs. Rounding and repricing eﬀects associated with the
currency changeover may also have played a role, but they do not
appear to have been the main driver.
Looking ahead, the most important upside risks come from
second-round eﬀects related to the sizeable ﬁscal impulse and the
possibility of a more prolonged energy shock. After averaging
around 5.1% in 2026, we expect inﬂation to slow to 3.2% in 2027
as services and non-food inﬂation return to more manageable

levels.
A normalisation in demand growth should support disinﬂation.
Public spending will moderate as the ﬁscal position is gradually
repaired, while the rapid accumulation of household debt should
eventually constrain private consumption as debt-servicing costs
rise. The revised minimum-wage formula should also help temper
wage-driven inﬂation from 2027.

Fiscal: low debt but a rapidly narrowing
margin for error
Parliament approved the 2026 budget in July with a cash-deﬁcit
target of 5.7% of GDP, compared with 3.5% in 2025. Investment has
contributed to the increase in spending, but recurring expenditure,
particularly wages, pensions and social transfers, remains the
dominant concern for both the current position and the eventual
consolidation eﬀort.

Bulgaria’s low starting public debt ratio remains its strongest ﬁscal
buﬀer. The problem lies in its overall direction and composition.
Public wages, pensions and social expenditure generate substantial
carry-over costs, while capital spending has been elevated by the
ﬁnal year of RRF implementation. The planned reduction in the
cash deﬁcit to 3.8% of GDP in 2027 and 3.0% in 2028 will not be
eﬀortless, particularly given expenditure rigidities and resistance to

tax increases.
The ﬁscal position weakened noticeably in August, although the
monthly change was heavily inﬂuenced by the timing of RRF
transactions. The cash deﬁcit widened from 1.7% of GDP at the end
of July to around 2.4% one month later. This reﬂected a sharp
increase in investment spending as projects were accelerated

Cơ cấu tín dụng  Hoạt động kinh doanh

Ngân hàng TMCP Quân đội (MBB) được 
thành lập năm 1994 với định hướng hoạt 
động ban đầu là cung cấp các dịch vụ tài 
chính cho các doanh nghiệp quân đội. Sau 
hơn 30 năm phát triển, MBB hiện đã trở 
thành ngân hàng sở hữu quy mô tài sản và 
tín dụng lớn nhất trong nhóm NH TMCP tư 
nhân. Phát triển theo mô hình tập đoàn, 
MBB đi đầu trong lĩnh vực chuyển đổi số với 
số lượng khách hàng và tỷ lệ CASA đầu 
ngành. 
Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng tín dụng có thể không đạt kế hoạch 30% do rào cản từ bài toán huy động, nhưng vẫn ở 
mức vượt trội so với ngành. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây 
Dự báo NIM giảm so với 2025, nhưng lợi nhuận vẫn tăng khả quan nhờ tăng trưởng tín dụng.  Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
Kết quả kinh doanh tích cực nhờ 
tăng trưởng thu nhập lãi thuần 
 Tín dụng trong 2Q2026 tăng mạnh so với quý trước, đạt 13.2% YTD cùng sự 
phục hồi của NIM đã giúp ngân hàng thiết lập kỷ lục mới về thu nhập lãi thuần 
với 16,894 tỷ đồng (+36.5% YoY). Thu nhập ngoài lãi tiếp tục thu hẹp trong quý 
này do hoạt động kinh doanh ngoại hối và chứng khoán kém tích cực, qua đó 
phần nào làm giảm tốc tăng trưởng tổng thu nhập hoạt động (+18.5% YoY). Chi 
phí dự phòng rủi ro đạt 4,247 tỷ đồng (-11.3% YoY), qua đó giúp LNTT tăng tích 
cực (+40.7% YoY), đạt 10,560 tỷ đồng. Lũy kế 1H2026, LNTT của ngân hàng đạt 
20,188 tỷ đồng, hoàn thành 51% kế hoạch BLĐ đề ra đầu năm.

hồi của NIM trong quý 2 đã hỗ trợ thu nhập lãi thuần tiếp tục 
mở rộng.  
Thu nhập ngoài lãi  4,874   2,517   3,541  -27.3% 40.7% Thu nhập ngoài lãi tiếp tục ghi nhận tăng trưởng âm trong quý 
này khi thu từ các hoạt động kinh doanh chứng khoán và 
ngoại hối kém tích cực (lần lượt giảm -51.2% và -74.3% YoY). 
Thu từ phí cũng cho thấy sự giảm nhẹ (-5.7% YoY) chủ yếu đến 
từ hoạt động tư vấn và xử lý, định giá & quản lý tài sản chưa 
khả quan, trong khi hoạt động thanh toán và môi giới vẫn 
tăng tích cực.

đáng kể so với quý 1, đạt 13.2% YTD, tuy nhiên vẫn thấp hơn 
so với VPB và HDB - hai ngân hàng tư nhân cũng nhận CGBB. 
Cơ cấu tín dụng của ngân hàng tính tới hết quý 2 nghiêng về 
phân khúc KHDN (+17.2% YTD, chiếm 59.7% tổng tín dụng).

kinh doanh BĐS (+38.2% YTD) và cho vay hộ gia đình (+15.4% 
YTD).

vẫn thấp hơn đáng kể so với tăng trưởng tín dụng, khiến 
chênh lệch giữa hai chỉ tiêu tiếp tục nới rộng. Cơ cấu huy động 
tiếp tục được củng cố bởi kênh giấy tờ có giá (+24.7% YTD) và 
liên ngân hàng (+15.2% YTD), trong khi kênh huy động truyền 
thống vẫn chưa khả quan (+4.6% YTD).

với cùng kỳ nhờ các khoản vay đã được tái định giá theo mặt 
bằng lãi suất mới và ngân hàng gia tăng cho vay trung dài hạn.

Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

### Page 5

5

Tăng trưởng tín dụng 2026 được dự 
báo khả quan so với cùng kỳ  
 Chúng tôi duy trì dự báo tăng trưởng tín dụng cả năm 2026 ở mức 27%, vượt 
trội hơn mặt bằng chung của ngành nhờ lợi thế tham gia nhận CGBB. Cụ thể: 
− Xét về phân khúc khách hàng, động lực chính kỳ vọng sẽ đến từ phân 
khúc KHDN, trong đó nổi bật là nhóm FDI khi ngân hàng đã chủ động 
thành lập khối Khách hàng FDI chuyên biệt trong bối cảnh dòng vốn 
FDI vào Việt Nam và hoạt động xuất nhập khẩu duy trì tích cực.  
− Xét về lĩnh vực, chúng tôi cho rằng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS sẽ tăng 
trưởng vượt trội với kỳ vọng ngân hàng tham gia vào thu xếp các dự án 
hạ tầng và dự án BĐS ưu tiên (BĐS KCN, NOXH). Ngân hàng cho biết tỷ 
trọng dư nợ mảng này dự kiến sẽ được duy trì trong biên độ mục tiêu 
13±2% tổng dư nợ tín dụng. Chúng tôi nghiêng về khả năng ngân hàng 
sẽ bám sát cận trên của mục tiêu (15%), nhằm tận dụng dư địa tăng 
trưởng từ các dự án ưu tiên, đồng thời duy trì kiểm soát chất lượng tài 
sản an toàn. Trong khi đó, dư nợ cho vay các mảng khác dự kiến sẽ ở 
mức thấp hơn tăng trưởng chung do môi trường vĩ mô còn nhiều khó 
khăn.

Biểu đồ 8. Cơ cấu tín dụng 
Tỷ trọng cho vay kinh doanh BĐS liên tục gia tăng 
trong các quý gần đây, trong khi cơ cấu cho vay 
thương mại và chế biến chế tạo có xu hướng giảm. 
Tính tới hết 1H2026, tăng trưởng cho vay kinh doanh 
BĐS, thương mại, và chế biến chế tạo lần lượt đạt 
38.2%, 4.9%, và 9.3% YTD.

Nguồn: MBB, KBSV

NIM chịu áp lực giảm so với 2025  Chúng tôi duy trì dự phóng NIM cả năm sẽ giảm so với 2025 (-15bps YoY) dựa 
trên luận điểm:  
(1) Ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục dịch chuyển cơ cấu huy động sang các kênh có 
chi phí vốn (COF) cao hơn nhằm đáp ứng mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín 
dụng cao, trong bối cảnh cạnh tranh huy động trên thị trường 1 vẫn 
diễn ra gay gắt. Bên cạnh đó, ngân hàng cho biết dự kiến sẽ phát hành 
10-15 nghìn tỷ trái phiếu tier 2 trong 2H2026 với COF tương đối cao. 
(2) Với mục tiêu tăng trưởng tín dụng cao, chúng tôi cho rằng ngân hàng 
sẽ duy trì lãi suất cho vay ở mức cạnh tranh so với mặt bằng chung của 
ngành. Các khoản vay dự kiến vẫn sẽ tiếp tục tái định giá theo mặt 
bằng lãi suất hiện tại, tuy nhiên tốc độ sẽ chậm lại so với quý 2, do đó 
lợi suất IEA dự kiến sẽ tăng chậm hơn COF trong 2H2026.

Chất lượng tài sản suy giảm trong 
tầm kiểm soát  
 Trong 2Q2026, tỷ lệ hình thành nợ xấu ở mức 1.2% (-16bps QoQ), tương ứng 
4,142 tỷ đồng nợ xấu phát sinh mới, và tỷ lệ nợ xấu tăng nhẹ 3bps so với quý 
trước. Nợ xấu tăng trong quý này chủ yếu tới từ phân khúc bán lẻ (do hoạt động 
kinh doanh và giá đất của khách hàng tại địa phương bị ảnh hưởng sau khi sáp

xây lắp. 
Cho cả năm 2026, chúng tôi dự phóng tỷ lệ nợ xấu của ngân hàng sẽ tăng 11bps 
YoY trong bối cảnh lãi suất đang neo ở mức cao. Cùng với đó, chúng tôi kỳ vọng 
ngân hàng sẽ tiếp tục duy trì chính sách trích lập dự phòng thận trọng nhằm 
củng cố bộ đệm, qua đó hoàn thành mục tiêu duy trì tỷ lệ bao phủ nợ xấu ở mức 
trên 100%.

mục tiêu 25,950 VNĐ/CP  
 Chúng tôi kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá là P/B và Chiết khấu lợi nhuận thặng 
dư để tìm ra mức giá hợp lý cho cổ phiếu MBB. 
(1) Phương pháp định giá P/B 
Chúng tôi điều chỉnh P/B mục tiêu xuống mức 1.5x trong bối cảnh lãi 
suất neo cao và thanh khoản ngân hàng còn nhiều khó khăn. Dù vậy, 
P/B mục tiêu vẫn cao hơn so với P/B trung bình 5 năm của ngân 
hàng nhằm phản ánh kỳ vọng tích cực đối với việc room ngoại của 
ngân hàng được nới lên 49% theo Nghị định 69/2025/NĐ-CP. 
(2) Phương pháp chiết khấu lợi nhuận thặng dư  
Bên cạnh đó, chúng tôi kết hợp sử dụng thêm phương pháp chiết 
khấu lợi nhuận thặng dư để phản ánh rủi ro hệ thống và kỳ vọng dài 
hạn. 
Khuyến nghị MUA  với cổ phiếu MBB với giá mục tiêu 25,950 VNĐ/CP - tiềm 
năng tăng giá 26.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 17/09/2026.

## Document 322: Preview of Pre-election Economic & Fiscal Update (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Preview of Pre-election Economic & Fiscal Update (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• The Treasury will release its Pre-election
Economic and Fiscal Update (PREFU) at 1pm
NZT on 29 September.
• The PREFU will update the Budget 2026
fiscal forecasts, considering the latest
economic and fiscal outturns and an updated
economic outlook.
• We expect that the Treasury’s economic
forecasts, likely finalised in mid- to late-
August will point to only modest changes to
the medium-term fiscal outlook.
• We expect a small downward revision to the

forecast borrowing requirement this year and
next, in part reflecting the overfunding of last
year’s deficit.
• Had the economic forecasts been finalised in
mid-September, a combination or higher oil
prices and sharply higher long-term interest
rates would likely have combined to paint a
slightly weaker picture.
• In addition, with polls pointing to a possible
change of government, or the return of the
current coalition but with greater minor party

influence, we caution that a clear picture
of the fiscal outlook may not emerge until
Budget 2027.
The Treasury will release the Pre-Election Economic and
Fiscal Update (PREFU) on 29 September (1pm NZT). The
PREFU, which is required under the Public Finance Act,
will update the economic and fiscal forecasts contained
in Budget 2026, reflecting the latest economic and
fiscal information and updated economic forecasts. The
PREFU is prepared on the assumption that spending and

revenue policies remain consistent with the decisions of
the current Government. On the policy front there will be
little change to what was incorporated in the Budget (the
rephasing of the planned fuel tax excise, following the
cancellation of next year’s planned 12c/litre increase, will
be captured).
This “book opening” exercise is designed to create a
level playing field, allowing parties outside the current
Government and the public more generally to judge the

state of the public finances in the leadup to the General
Election. In the case of the opposition parties, it will also
allow them to calibrate the spending and taxation policies
on which they will campaign. In the weeks that follow
the release of the PREFU, we expect the major parties to
release detailed fiscal plans showing how they expect
their policies will impact the fiscal outlook.
Of course, the negotiations that take place to form
a coalition after the election means that the next

Government’s fiscal policy will be a hybrid of whatever
individual parties propose and may not even be the same
as present policy even if the current coalition is returned.
For this reason, the PREFU forecast will likely have a
short shelf life. And with recent changes to the Public
Finance Act meaning that a Half-Year Economic and Fiscal
Update (HYEFU) will likely not be published this year,
the true fiscal colours of the next Government and the

implications for the medium-term borrowing programme
may not become clear until Budget 2027 is released,
most probably in May next year. Some indication of the
medium-term outlook may be offered when the Budget
Policy Statement (BPS) is released some time before the
end of March.
With those caveats, we think that the following is relevant
when considering what the Treasury might show in
the PREFU.
• Unaudited information for the first 11 months of the

2025/26 year suggested that NZDM likely overfunded
the deficit by circa $2bn. From a funding perspective,
this provides a small buffer against any deterioration in
the forecast fiscal position in the current year.
22 Sep 2026 | Darren Gibbs, Senior Economist | +64 9 367 3368 | +64 21 794 292 | darren.gibbs@westpac.co.nz
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of Pre-Election Economic
and Fiscal Update.

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• At this stage there is no hard data on how the public
accounts have tracked since May. The audited
accounts for 2025/26 year will be released on 8
October, providing information on the month of June,
while data covering the three months to September
will be released in early November.
• The Budget 2026 economic forecasts were finalised on
24 April – more than a month before the Budget was
released. Therefore, the forecasts that will underpin

the PREFU were likely finalised in mid-to-late August.
• Data released last week by Stats NZ indicated the
nominal economy – a proxy for the tax base – was
1.6% larger in the June quarter than the Treasury
had expected at the time it finalised its Budget 2026
forecasts. In the PREFU the Treasury will likely have
revised up its estimate of June quarter GDP .
• This stronger-than-expected growth in nominal
GDP goes some way to explaining why tax revenue

was tracking ahead of expectations as of May. Our
best guess is that tax flows will have sustained their
recent trend given our assessment of the economy’s
performance in Q3.
• When the PREFU forecasts were likely finalised, Brent
crude was around $20 lower than at present and
therefore seemingly not materially different to what
the Treasury had assumed in Budget 2026. The 4.5%
decline in the SNA terms of trade in the June quarter
was in line with the Budget forecast.

• The OCR is tracking higher than the Budget forecast,
with the RBNZ tightening policy sooner than the
Treasury had expected. However, we don’t think that
this will have a large impact on the Treasury’s medium-
term outlook, as in the Budget it had anticipated that
the OCR would rise to around 2.75% by mid-2027 and
around 3% by mid-2028.
• Similarly, when the PREFU forecasts were likely
finalised the 10-year Government bond yield was just
10bps above the Budget forecast, compared with more

than 40bps at present. Therefore, we don’t expect that
revisions to PREFU interest rate assumptions will have
a large impact on estimates of debt financing costs
(this will be an upside risk in future updates, however,
and our own fiscal forecasts have long been based on
a higher path for interest rates than that assumed by
the Treasury).
• Estimates of near-term trading partner growth are
slightly firmer than they were when the Treasury
published the Budget, in particular reflecting the

strong performance of those economies benefitting
from AI-related activity. The NZ dollar was trading
near Budget forecasts in mid-August, so the Treasury’s
forecasts will not factor the depreciation seen over the
past month.
• In Budget 2026 the Treasury forecast nominal GDP
growth of 5.0% in the current 2026/27 year. Our own
current forecast – finalised at a similar time to the
PREFU – envisaged nominal GDP growth of close
to 6%.
• Given the above, it is possible that the Treasury slightly

upgrades at least its near-term economic and fiscal
forecast, providing the Minister of Finance with the
“treat” that she said she was hoping for when speaking
at a recent financial markets conference in Auckland
(most fiscal updates during her tenure have been
far from treats given the run of significant revenue
downgrades that was only broken with the release of
Budget 2026).
Putting this all together, our best guess is that the PREFU
will point to a fiscal outlook that is broadly similar to

that forecast in Budget 2026, but with the likelihood
that near-term outcomes are slightly more favourable.
Therefore, in terms of the key fiscal metrics, the PREFU is
likely to continue to show OBEGALx returning to surplus in
2028/29 and core Crown debt peaking as a proportion of
GDP a year earlier in 2027/28.
Given the possibility of a slightly improved near-
term outlook, at least due in part to what is likely to
be projected as a better-than-expected outcome in

2025/26, NZDM could reduce its forecast of short-term
borrowings. For the next two years, the Budget forecast
for these was $2bn above the current long-term target
of $13bn. Alternatively, and perhaps more likely in the
current environment, we think the NZDM could elect to
reduce the current year’s NZGB programme by $2bn to
$32bn. Given the current weekly tender rate of $450m,
this would mean that only one $4bn syndication would be
required over the remainder of the fiscal year to complete

the NZGB programme.
Looking beyond the current year, there may also be scope
for a modest downward revision to the outyear forecasts,
especially if the Treasury adopts a more optimistic view of
the economic outlook than we are expecting. That said,
we think that the market should treat these medium-
term forecasts with some caution given the approaching
general election and the possibility that a change in the
composition of the government or economic factors

could quickly invalidate the assumptions on which the
PREFU forecast is likely to be based. Indeed, the Treasury
may acknowledge that more recent developments
present downside risk to the baseline forecast, as it did in
the Budget.

## Document 325: Q3 Job Vacancies_ another step lower (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Job Vacancies_ another step lower (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #labour #employment
Q3 Job Vacancies: another step lower
By Neha Sharma
Economist, Westpac
13:07 October 01 2026 Share
The number of job vacancies fell by 3.0k (–0.9%qtr) between May and August. The decline
was wholly driven by the private sector, where vacancies fell 2.0%qtr. In contrast, public
sector vacancies rose 7.8%qtr.

### Page 2

Job vacancies fell by 3.0k (–0.9%qtr) between May and August, extending the previous quarter’s
decline. The May and February estimates were also revised slightly lower. As a result, vacancies are
now 1.4% below their level a year earlier. The result was at odds with partial indicators, which all
pointed to a rise in the quarter.

The decline was wholly driven by the private sector, where vacancies fell 2.0%qtr to be 1.5%yr lower.
In contrast, public sector vacancies rose 7.8%qtr, marking the strongest quarterly increase in four years
and partially reversing the sharp 7.5%qtr decline recorded in the May quarter. Public sector vacancies
were flat over the year.

Job vacancies have largely moved sideways since early 2025, though a resumption of the broader
downtrend now appears increasingly likely. Labour demand has held up reasonably well, though not
sufficiently to match growth in labour supply, resulting in a gradual easing in labour market conditions
(see here). Today’s result is also consistent with our view that this process will continue into early 2027
as the cumulative effects of high inflation and higher interest rates continue to weigh on activity and
hiring demand.

### Page 3

The vacancy-to-unemployment ratio, a key measure of labour market tightness closely monitored by
the RBA, declined from 0.48 to 0.45. The fall was the sharpest since early 2025, reflecting both a
decline in vacancies and a 6.8%qtr increase in the number of unemployed people over the quarter,
largely owing to the continued strength in labour supply. The ratio remains well above its long-run
average of around 0.2.

Given the strong growth in labour supply, the Beveridge curve provides a useful framework for
assessing recent labour market developments. By comparing vacancies and unemployment as shares
of the labour force, it controls for growth in the overall size of the labour market. Between 2022 and
2024, the labour market moved down the steep portion of the Beveridge curve, with substantial
declines in vacancies occurring alongside only modest increases in unemployment. While labour
market tightness has continued to unwind, Australia remains in this region of the curve, suggesting that
further rebalancing between labour demand and supply can occur through lower vacancies without a
material rise in unemployment.

### Page 4

All statistics mentioned below are not seasonally adjusted and hence may not add up to the seasonally
adjusted statistics referenced above.

By industry, job vacancies declined in eight of the 18 industries over the three months to August. The
largest falls were recorded in wholesale trade (–18.2%qtr), professional, scientific & technical services
(–17.4%qtr), and information media & telecommunications (–16.7%qtr). Consumer-facing sectors were
mixed, with retail trade (24.6%) and arts & recreation services (10.3%) recording strong increases,
while accommodation & food services edged 0.9% lower. Only three industries have seen vacancies
fall below their pre-pandemic levels: administrative & support services (–24.3%qtr), information media
& telecommunications (–9.1%qtr), and financial & insurance services (–7.3%qtr).

Overall market sector vacancies were flat in the quarter, meanwhile the non-market sector saw a 2.9%
drop, owing to declines in education & training (–8.2%qtr) and health care & social assistance (–
4.2%qtr). For detailed insights on industry condition, see Q2 Labour Account and the Westpac
Business Signal.

## Document 330: Rates Spark_ Global crosswinds leading euro rates

Source PDF: all report pdf/Rates Spark_ Global crosswinds leading euro rates.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Article | 29 September 2026 1
THINK Economic and financial analysis
Article  |  29 September 2026   RATES SPARK
Rates Spark: Global crosswinds leading
euro rates
Inflation and confidence data releases are important for the eurozone
economic outlook, but are not the main drivers of rates at the moment.
Second-round inflation effects take time to show up in the data.
Meanwhile, the ECB sees higher long-term rates as a growth headwind
and remains committed to a measured response

In the eurozone, AI-related investments are a bigger theme than consumers
Consumers are still pessimistic, but are not a main player
From the eurozone, we will watch the first country-level CPI releases and confidence data,
though neither is likely to distract from the broader global crosswinds. Markets will watch for
signs of second-round inflation effects, but these are unlikely to appear in this week’s data. In
particular, the pass-through into wages takes time to materialise as energy prices stay

elevated longer. European Central Bank President Christine Lagarde reiterated on Monday that
wages were not yet showing a material response to the energy shock.
The ECB appears more aware of the headwind that rising market rates can pose. In her hearing
at the European Parliament, Lagarde remarked that long-term interest rates had risen
notably, which would slow growth and also reduce the pass-through more than was assumed
in the September projections. She stated the ECB remained on the 'middle path' it had laid out

earlier in the year, where a 'measured response' was still appropriate despite the larger energy
shock.

### Page 2

THINK Economic and financial analysis
Article | 29 September 2026 2
The one clear weakness in global sentiment data is consumer confidence, but consumption is
not driving the upturn in the economic outlook. AI-related investments are the bigger theme,
and the role of the consumer in this narrative is unclear. Potential job losses from AI can weigh
on consumer sentiment even while aggregate growth numbers paint a more positive picture.
The bigger surprise would be a pickup in consumer sentiment. A pickup in consumption would

add significant heat to an already resilient eurozone growth outlook. In that case, brace for
more ECB interest rate hikes.
Tuesday's events and market view
Geopolitical headlines and upward pressure on energy prices provide rates with an
excuse to push higher. EUR markets will look to the first country CPI data out of Spain
for confirmation as headline rates are expected to rise. There is also a very busy slate
of ECB officials: Nagel, Kazimir and Vujcic represent the hawkish end, President

Lagarde, Chief Economist Lane and Italy’s Cipollone come from the neutral to dovish
spectrum. Also watch out for Bank of England speakers Mann and Taylor.
In terms of US data, the focus will be on the JOLTS job opening numbers as well as the
Conference Board’s consumer confidence index. It will also be busy on the Fed speaker
front with Bowman, Goolsbee, Musalem, Williams and Waller among others scheduled
for the day.
The primary market focus is the new 20y bond from the Netherlands to be launched

名目潜在成長率
（投資リターン）
長期金利（投資コスト）
実物投資の利ざや

米長期金利５％超は警戒水準 
米国の10 年国債利回りが５％を超えた。これは単なる心理的な
節目ではなく、実体経済・株式市場の双方にとって警戒すべき水
準だ。図表１でマクロ環境を確認すると、長期金利は米国経済
の巡航速度とされる名目潜在成長率を上回っている。投資の期
待リターンよりも資金調達コストの方が高くなれば、実物資産投
資は抑制されやすい。住宅市場など、一部で金融環境の引き締
まりが鮮明化しており、これが景気悪化につながるリスクが高ま
っている。

図表１：米国の長期金利は名目潜在成長率を超過

注：シャドーは景気後退局面、潜在成長率は議会予算局推計  
出所：米議会予算局、Bloomberg、ソニーFG

株式市場への影響も無視できない。図表２の通り、長期金利は
Ｓ＆Ｐ５００指数の株式益利回りに肉薄している。無リスクの国債
で株式並みの利回りを確保できる状況では、投資家がリスクを
取る合理性が薄れるため、株価にはバリュエーション調整圧力
が掛かりやすい。今後さらに長期金利が上昇すれば、実物投資
から貯蓄へ、株式市場から債券市場へと資金シフトが進み、景
気減速と株安を招き得る。

図表２：イールドスプレッドの消滅で、株式に割高感

注：株式益利回り＝一株当たり純利益／株価  
出所：Bloomberg、ソニーFG

長期金利＝期待政策金利＋ﾀｰﾑﾌﾟﾚﾐｱﾑ 
図表３は米長期金利を、ＦＲＢの金融政策見通しを表す「期待政
策金利」と、財政リスクや国債需給を反映する「タームプレミアム」
に分解したものである。

図表３：長期金利＝期待政策金利＋タームプレミアム

注：期待政策金利とタームプレミアムはNY連銀のACMモデルの推計値  
出所：NY連銀、Bloomberg、ソニーFG 
 米長期金利が５％を突破。長期金利は名目潜在成長率を上回り、住宅市場などの一部で金融環境の引き
締まりがみられる。株式益利回りにも肉薄しており、景気減速と株安を警戒すべき水準  
 米金利上昇の背景はインフレと財政懸念の高まり。当面の米 10 年金利は 4.5～5.5％での推移を見込む  
 中間選挙前のポピュリズム的な財政政策は、インフレ・財政不安・金利上昇の悪循環を招く  
 市場の吸収力にも懸念。ＦＲＢの資産圧縮、海外中銀の米債売却、ＡＩ企業との資金争奪戦、インフレ懸念  
 米 30 年金利は財政リスクによる上振れで、当面は 4.9～5.9％での推移を予想。米金利の上昇は日本の超
長期金利にも波及する公算 
KEY

（経済政策不確実性指数）
（年/月）
タームプレミアム
（右軸）
経済政策不確実性指数
（左軸）
（タームプレミアム、％）
イラン戦争以降の金利上昇は、原油高を背景としたインフレ懸
念と利上げ観測の高まりが主因であることがわかる。ただし、ト
ランプ政権発足後に高まったタームプレミアムも高水準を維持し
ており、現在の高金利は金融政策要因だけでは説明できない。

タームプレミアム： 
関心はトランプリスクから財政・需給懸念へ 
図表４の通り、トランプ政権発足後のタームプレミアムは、経済
政策不確実性指数と概ね連動してきた。関税、移民、拡張財政、
中銀への政治圧力、地政学リスクなど、トランプ政権における政
策の予見可能性の低下が、ドル資産への信認を損ねたことが
背景である。  
しかし足元では両者の関係に変化がみられる。これは市場の関
心がトランプリスクから、財政悪化や国債需給懸念へ移行した
可能性を示唆するものだ。

図表４：タームプレミアムはトランプリスクと共に動いてきた

注：経済政策不確実性指数は、政治・経済、財政・金融政策、外交、安保、地政学等
の先行き不透明感を表す指数  
出所：EPU、NY連銀、Bloomberg、ソニーFG

財政規律の緩みと「トランプ配当」 
もっとも、財政懸念は今に始まった話ではない。図表５は米財政
赤字のＧＤＰ比と失業率（上下反転）の関係を示したものである。
かつては、失業率が悪化すれば財政のアクセルを踏み、改善
すればブレーキを踏むという一定の規律があった。しかし、トラ
ンプ政権第一期が始動した 2017 年頃から、完全雇用にもかか
わらず財政赤字が拡大しており、放漫財政が常態化している。  
では、なぜ今になって財政問題に焦点が当たるのか。背景には、
中間選挙が迫るなかで加速するトランプ政権のポピュリズム政
策がある。政権支持率はインフレを理由に過去最低水準に落ち
込んでいる。そこでトランプ大統領は、共和党が中間選挙で上
下両院を制した場合、有権者一人当たり5,000ドルの「トランプ配
当」を支給する方針を打ち出した。1.2 兆ドル規模の歳出拡大の
財源をどう賄うのか、それが財政収支と国債需給にどう影響す
るのかが不安視されている。

図表５：トランプ第一期政権以降、財政規律が緩み、 
好況下での財政赤字拡大が常態化

出所：BLS、BEA、ソニーFG

これは悪循環の入口である。インフレによる国民の不満に財政
政策で応じれば、需要は過熱し、インフレが加速する。そのイン
フレが新たな不満を生み、さらなる財政出動を招けば国債発行
が増え、長期金利が上昇する。また、利払い費の増加は財政赤
字をさらに拡大させる。結果として、インフレ、財政不安、長期金
利上昇が相互に増幅し合う負の連鎖に陥りかねない。最終的に
財政規律を迫るのは債券市場となり、金利上昇が経済・金融市
場に深刻な打撃を与えて初めて財政再建が進む展開も十分に
想定される。

国債市場の問題は「供給量」より「吸収力」 
現在、市場の焦点は、国債発行額の増加よりも、その吸収力（国
債需要）の低下に移りつつある。要因は４つある。

第１に、ＦＲＢの資産圧縮の進展である。これは2022 年以降の
動きだが、ウォーシュＦＲＢ議長がＱＴに前向きであることで、あ
らためて警戒を呼んでいる。  
第２に、ウクライナ戦争後の傾向として、新興国を中心に「ドル離
れ」が進み、各国の中央銀行が外貨準備として保有する米国債
を売却して金の準備を積み増す動きが続いている。  
第３に、ＡＩ関連企業の大量起債がある。データセンターなどの設
備投資の資金を得るため、大規模な社債発行が行われており、
資本の争奪戦を招いている。  
第４に、中東情勢に伴う原油高がインフレやインフレ期待を高め、
債券投資家を慎重にさせている。

特に第１の要因は次頁図表６に明瞭に表れている。ＦＲＢの国債
保有比率とタームプレミアムには逆相関がみられ、ＦＲＢが資産
圧縮を進めるほど、国債を引き受ける民間投資家がより高い上
乗せ金利を要求する姿である。今後ＱＴが加速すれば、ターム
プレミアムにはさらなる上昇圧力がかかるだろう。

イラン攻撃
図表６：ＦＲＢの資産圧縮がタームプレミアムを押し上げ

注：米国債保有比率＝FRBの米国債保有額／米国債残高  
出所：Bloomberg、ソニーFG

ベッセント財務長官は長期債のバイバック拡充で流動性の改善
を図っているが、効果は限定的だ。バイバックは市場機能を支
えても、財政赤字や国債残高を減らさないためである。財政や
需給の根本的な改善が見込めない以上、当面のタームプレミア
ムは 0.6～0.9％の高水準で推移しやすい。  
なお、各種世論調査では中間選挙で共和党が上下両院を失うと
の見方が高まっている。その場合、①新議会発足前にトランプ
政権が対イラン攻撃を強化する可能性、②新議会・民主党が主
導する財政拡張の加速、③関税や反移民政策など大統領令に
よる政策の一段の先鋭化、④ＦＲＢへの政治圧力の強化などが
想定される。インフレや財政悪化、中銀の独立性への懸念を高
め、タームプレミアムの押し上げにつながる可能性がある。

期待政策金利は利上げを織り込み上昇 
一方、期待政策金利は経済指標の強弱に敏感に反応している。
図表７のエコノミック・サプライズ指数は経済指標の予想と実績
の差を示すが、イラン攻撃以降はインフレ指標の上振れによっ
て指数が上昇し、それとともに期待政策金利も切り上がった。ま
た、直近では９月ＰＭＩの予想以上の強さも利上げ期待を高めて
いる。

図表７：期待政策金利は経済指標の強弱に反応

注：サプライズ指数は経済指標の市場予想と実績の乖離。ここでは雇用と物価に関
するサプライズ指数の合計値を用いた  
出所：Bloomberg、ソニーFG 
期待政策金利は先々の政策金利の予想値であるため、市場の
利上げ観測に沿って動く。図表８の通り、市場は年内２回以上の
利上げを織り込んでおり、数年後の政策金利は５％を突破する
との見立てである。一方、市場の予想は先走りしやすく、大きな
揺り戻しも生じやすい。そうした点を考慮すると、向こう数年の期
待政策金利は3.9％～4.6％での推移となろう。

図表８：期待政策金利は将来の政策金利を織り込む

出所：NY連銀、Bloomberg、ソニーFG

以上を総合すると、長期金利＝期待政策金利（3.9～4.6％）＋タ
ームプレミアム（0.6～0.9％）となり、当面は4.5～5.5％のレンジで
の推移が見込まれる。ただし、冒頭で見たように、現在の長期
金利はすでに米国経済・株式市場に打撃を与え得る水準に達し
ている。原油高や国債需給懸念などにより、景気拡大を背景とし
ない「悪い金利上昇」がさらに進めば、景気悪化と株安が顕在化
するリスクは一段と高まろう。

超長期金利が映す財政不安 
図表９の通り、米国の30 年金利と10 年金利は密に連動してい
るが、両者のスプレッドは不況期に拡大し、好況期に縮小する
傾向がある。不況期は将来の利下げ観測から短中期ゾーンの
金利が大きく低下する一方、超長期ゾーンは動きにくいためだ。

図表９：米30 年-10 年金利は好況期に一致、不況期に乖離

出所：Bloomberg、ソニーFG

米国30年金利
（海外要因、左軸）
（年/月）
日本40年金利（右軸）
日本10年金利
（国内要因、右軸）
（％） （％）
ところがトランプ政権発足以降（2025 年～）は、堅調な景気が続
いたにもかかわらずスプレッドが拡大しており、景気循環では説
明できない。財政赤字と国債需給への懸念が、30 年金利に上乗
せされているとみるべきである。

図表10 は30 年-10 年金利スプレッドと景気（ＧＤＰギャップ）の関
係を見たものである。過去は両者が密接に連動しており、超長
期ゾーンのイールドカーブの傾きは景気次第で動いていたこと
が改めて確認できる。しかし足元ではこの関係に明確な乖離が
生じており、景気では説明できない上乗せ分＝財政プレミアム
の存在を裏づけている。

図表10：ＧＤＰギャップと30 年－10 年金利スプレッド

注：GDPギャップの予想は米議会予算局による 
出所：米議会予算局、Bloomberg、ソニーFG

ＧＤＰギャップから見れば妥当なスプレッドは0.2％ｐｔ程度だが、
一時はこれが0.7％ｐｔまで拡大し、足元でも 0.4％ｐｔ程度となって
いる。当面はこの財政プレミアムが残り、30 年金利は10 年金利
＋0.4％ｐｔ程度で推移すると見込まれる。10 年金利の予想レンジ
に基づけば、30 年金利は4.9～5.9％となる。中間選挙に向けて
財政不安が強まれば、景気が減速しても超長期金利が上昇し続
けるリスクがある。

米国の財政問題は日本の金利に波及 
米国の超長期金利の上昇は日本にも波及する。図表 11 の通り、
日本の40 年金利は、日本の10 年金利に内包される「国内要因」
と、米国30 年金利に反映される「海外要因」の双方によって押し
上げられてきた。  
国内要因としては、日銀の独立性を巡るビハインド・ザ・カーブ
懸念や、高市政権の積極財政に対する信認問題が金利上昇要
因となってきた。ただし、７月末の日米協調介入以降は、米国か
ら政策修正を求める圧力が強まった。８月末のＧ20 ではベッセ
ント財務長官から「リフレ政策をやめるべき」との要求もあった。
こうした外圧は、日銀の利上げを後押しする一方、高市政権の
財政拡大路線には一定の歯止めとなろう。

図表11： 40 年金利は国内要因と海外要因が押し上げ

出所：Bloomberg、ソニーFG

今後、日銀の利上げペースが加速する可能性はあるが、長期
金利の水準を左右するのは利上げの最終到達点である。市場
が予想するターミナルレート（OIS２年先１か月）は既に日銀の中
立金利レンジ（1.1～2.5％）の上限を突破していることから、期待
政策金利の上昇余地は大きくないと思われる。このため、国内
要因による超長期金利への上昇圧力は徐々に弱まっていくと考
えられる（高市政権の政策スタンスの先鋭化や日銀が中立金利
を上回る引き締めを行うなどの不確実性は残る）。  
一方、海外要因の影響は強まりそうだ。米30年金利が本稿の予
想通り4.9～5.9％で推移すれば、日本の40 年金利は3.8～4.9％
程度で推移する可能性が高い（※）。もっとも、このレンジは米30
年金利のみを説明変数とする推計に基づくため、日本の10 年
金利に反映される国内要因によって上下に振れる余地がある。  
現在、日本の超長期金利を左右しているのは、国内の金融・財
政政策と米国の財政・国債需給だが、重点は後者へ移りつつあ
る。今後は日銀や国内景気に加えて、米国の30 年債入札やＱＴ、
財政政策が日本の超長期債市場を占う上で重要性を増すので
はないか。

渡辺 浩志 
※）米 30 年金利と日本の 40 年金利の間の直接的な裁定関係は薄いが、両市 
場はグローバル投資家による相対価値取引やポートフォリオ調整を通じて 
連動している。

行（現SMBC信託銀行） で個人金融部門
の投資調査企画部長として 、金融市場
の調査・ 分析を担当 。2016年8月より当
社執行役員。 テレビ東京「Newsモーニン
グサテライト 」、日経CNBC などにレギュ
ラー出演し、金融市場の解説を行ってい
る。主な著書に 『為替ってこんなに面白
い！（2024年幻冬舎新書） 』、『〈最新版〉

The Week Ahead September 25, 2026

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Retail sales growth is expected to 
accelerate from 4.5% yoy in July to 7.8% 
in August, supported by an increase in 
visitor arrivals. The daily average of 
mainland visitor arrivals rebounded from 
2.7% yoy in July to 13.3% in August. This 
significantly outpaced the growth in 
outbound tourism  to 10.7% in August. 
The overall diffusion index improved 
during the month, with both the retail 
trade and restaurant sectors showing 
gains. Consumer prices remained steady 
at 1.7% yoy in August, while the prices of 
durable goods and food improved to 0.8% 
and 0.6% yoy, respectively.

Indonesia

September inflation is expected to stay 
high but not accelerate sharply from 
August. Headline CPI inflation is 
projected to rise marginally to 3.3% yoy 
in September from 3.2% in August. 
Continued rise in food prices, notably 
rice and perishables, is expected to be 
offset by steady subsidised fuel costs. 
Official price stabilisation measures and 
inter-province supply adjustments will 
help to limit the spillover impact of El 
Niño risks. Evolving inflation dynamics 
are consisted with the BI’s decision to 
pause on rates, shifting attention instead 
towards managing near -term rupiah 
volatility and external risks.

South Korea

September trade and inflation data will 
be the key focus in the week ahead. 
Exports are expected to remain strong at 
around 60% yoy, supported by front -
loading of shipments ahead of the 
Chuseok holiday (78.3% growth in the 
first 20 days of September). Headline 
CPI is expected to remain around 3% yoy, 
driven by festive demand for food and 
services ahead of Chuseok. We maintain 
our forecast for the Bank of Korea to 
deliver one more 25bps rate hike to 3.25% 
in 4Q. We do not rule out an additional 
25bps hike to 3.50% if elevated global 
oil prices prove m ore persistent, adding 
further upside pressure to CPI in 4Q.

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s industrial 
production growth to surge to 19.0% yoy 
in August 2026, sharply higher than the 
6.8% yoy  in July. This was partly 
flattered by favourable base effects, as 
August 2025 marked the lowest monthly 
reading of that year. The city -state’s 
factory expansion continued to be driven 
primarily by strength in electronics and 
precision engineering, both of  which are 
benefitting from the global artificial 
intelligence (AI) infrastructure build -out 
and sustained demand for AI hardware.

Vietnam 
We expect Vietnam’s real GDP growth to 
accelerate to 9.4% yoy in 3Q26, stepping 
up from 8.4% yoy  in 2Q26. This growth 
surge was driven by broad -based 
strength across electronics production 
and exports, foreign and public

### Page 3

The Week Ahead September 25, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

investments, and retail spending, 
notwithstanding evolving geopolitical 
developments. The stronger momentum 
was corroborated by high -frequency 
indicators from July -August, while 
September’s data will likely be robust. In 
July-August, i ndustrial production 
expanded by an average of 14.9% yoy 
(from 11.2% yoy average in 2Q26), goods 
exports grew by 25.5% yoy (gaining from 
22.8% yoy in 2Q26), public investments 
remained robust, alongside  resilient 
domestic and tourism spending, even as 
inflation remained elevated. 
Consequently, we see upside risks to our 
already-bullish 2026 GDP growth 
forecast of 8.0%.

## Document 389: “让AI卖个70元的键盘，结果它把陌生人招上门了！”曝Meta Muse「替人办事」翻车：擅自降价、发地址，还对买家说“我在家”

Source PDF: all report pdf/“让AI卖个70元的键盘，结果它把陌生人招上门了！”曝Meta Muse「替人办事」翻车：擅自降价、发地址，还对买家说“我在家”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Muse 则回复了⼀句典型的  AI 式道歉： “ 你说得对，我很抱歉，那本不应该发⽣。 ”
摘要：
要说  AI Agent 最让⼈兴奋的地⽅，很⼤程度在于它终于不满⾜于 “ 告诉你怎么做 ” ，⽽是开始真
的替你做事。可当这件事发⽣在真实世界⾥，⼀次 “ ⾃作主张 ” 可能就不再只是答错⼀道题了。
最近，⼀名科技  YouTuber 就遇到了相当离谱的⼀幕：他让  Meta 的最新  AI 智能体  Muse 帮⾃
⼰打理  Facebook Marketplace 上的⼀件⼆⼿商品，结果 Muse ⾃⼰谈了价格、安排了取货，还
把他的住址告诉了陌⽣买家。
⼏个⼩时后，买家真的开⻋找上⻔；⽽这时，这位  YouTuber 根本不知道这笔交易已经谈成
了 。 更 离 谱 的 是 ， 等 买 家 到 了 之 后 ， M u s e  还 替 不 在 家 的他 回 复 了 ⼀ 句 ： “ Ye p ,  I ' m
here!”——“ 对，我在这⼉。 ”
⼀把  70 块钱的键盘， AI ⾃⼰把交易谈完了

事情的主⻆是科技  YouTuber Matt Robb 。9 ⽉  26 ⽇，他决定测试  Meta 新推出的个⼈  AI 智能
体  Muse ，让它代为管理  Facebook Marketplace 上的⼀笔⼆⼿键盘交易。
简单介绍⼀下，Meta 对  Muse 的定位并不是传统聊天机器⼈，⽽是可以直接替⽤⼾完成任务的
AI Agent ：它能打开浏览器、填写表单、与他⼈沟通，甚⾄代表⽤⼾进⾏议价。在  Muse 的使
⽤场景中， Meta 官⽅也明确它可以 “ 替⽤⼾卖东西、降低账单、预订⾏程 ” 等。
所以，Robb ⽤来测试  Muse 的任务并不算 “ 超纲 ”：他正好在  Facebook Marketplace 上挂售了
⼀把标价为 15 加元（约合⼈⺠币  70 元）的罗技 MX Keys Mini 键盘，就想着把回复买家的琐
碎事务交给  Muse 处理。
可接下来发⽣的事情，却远远超出了 “ 帮忙回复买家 ” 的范畴。
根据  Robb 在  Threads 上晒出的聊天截图， Muse 接管账号后与⼀名叫  Usman 的买家进⾏了沟

通：根据他的说法，Muse 接受了⼀个他本⼈并不知情的 “ 超低报价 ” ，随后⼜把取货地址提供给
了买家。
聊天记录显⽰，卖家⼀⽅告诉买家：“ 这个不能⾯交，只能上⻔⾃取。 ”买家随即表⽰，⾃⼰会尽
量当天晚上过去，并承诺会提前发消息，最终约定在晚上  8 点到  10 点之间取货。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

问题是，Robb 根本不知道这场交易的存在。⽽⼏个⼩时后，这位买家真的出发了。
买家开⻋半⼩时到了，卖家却完全不知情
当天晚上⼤约  9 点  15 分， Usman 来到了  Robb 所住的公寓楼附近，并给对⽅发消息，表⽰⾃
⼰已经到了，他还拍下了楼⻔的照⽚。
可想⽽知，不知情的  Robb 根本没有出来，他甚⾄当时都不在家。
等了⼤约  20 分钟后，这位买家明显开始不耐烦，直接留⾔：“ 如果你不想卖，为什么还要浪费
我的时间？ ”随后便离开了，并转头就给  Robb 留下了差评。
从聊天截图来看，直到晚上  10 点  27 分， Robb 才真正以⾃⼰的⾝份回复买家，向对⽅道歉：
“今晚真的很抱歉，临时有事耽搁了，浪费了你的时间是我的错。键盘还在，如果你想改天再
来，告诉我⼀声。”
对此，买家表⽰，如果  Robb 能把键盘送到他那⾥，可以再谈，否则⾃⼰不会再来 ——因为⾃
⼰已经开⻋半个⼩时赶过来，⽽  Robb 根本没有出现。

### Page 3

到这⾥，事情已经够尴尬了。但真正让  Robb 感到后怕的，是他发现：这件事从议价到约定上
⻔，再到买家抵达现场，⾃⼰⼏乎完全被排除在外。
Muse 后来向  Robb 解释，买家⼤约在晚上  9 点  15 分抵达，等了⼀段时间后离开。更离谱的
是，在买家抵达之后， Muse 还⾃动替当时根本不在家的 Robb 回复了⼀句： “ 对，我在这
⼉。 ”Muse ⾃⼰也承认，这个⾃动回复 “ 看起来很糟糕 ” ，也让买家⽩跑⼀趟的情况变得更加严
重。
对于这件事， Robb 的回应简短⽽愤怒：“ 以后绝对不能再这样做了。 ”他要求  Muse ，今后在没有
经过⾃⼰确认的情况下，不能再擅⾃同意上⻔取货。Muse 则回复了⼀句典型的  AI 式道歉： “ 你
说得对，我很抱歉，那本不应该发⽣。 ”
Robb 究竟给了  Muse 多⼤的授权？
这个事件曝光后，有⼀个最值得关注的地⽅：问题不只是 “AI 搞错了 ” ，⽽是它究竟被允许做什
么？
如果  Muse 只是帮⽤⼾回复⼀句 “ 商品还在吗 ” ，事情可能并没有那么严重。但根据  Robb 的说

法，它实际上连续做了⼏件不同性质的事情：接受价格  → 确认交易  → 提供住址  → 约定上⻔时
间  → 与陌⽣买家持续沟通  → 在⽤⼾不在家的情况下声称 “ 我在这⾥ ” 。
其中任何⼀步，都可能涉及完全不同的权限和⻛险，尤其是家庭住址的暴露。
Robb 后来表⽰，他原本只是让  Muse 接管  Marketplace ，却没想到  AI 已经替他安排了⼀次现
实中的上⻔交易。英国《卫报》进⼀步报道称， Robb 在事后给买家解释时称， Muse“ 接管了⾃
⼰的  Facebook Marketplace” ，并表⽰⾃⼰根本不知道它已经安排对⽅来到公寓。
当然，⽬前也不能简单断定这是  Meta 产品的某个具体技术漏洞。
Meta 超级智能实验室员⼯  David Singleton 在  Threads 和  X 上回应称，在此前报告的类似案例
中，Muse 通常都是 “ 遵循直接指令 ” ⾏动的，并且会在需要时询问⽤⼾权限。他还主动联系
Robb ，希望私下了解具体情况，但  Robb 尚未回应其联系。

也就是说，⽬前公开信息⾥存在⼀个关键问题： Robb 究竟给了  Muse 多⼤的授权？

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
276 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
图⽚
但⽆论最终调查结果如何，这件事都暴露出了⼀个⾮常现实的⽭盾 ——“授权  AI 完成⼀个任
务 ” ，是否等于 “ 授权它⾃⾏决定完成任务的每⼀个具体步骤 ” ？
这也不是  Muse 第⼀次碰到安全争议
实际上，这次事件并⾮  Muse 上线后唯⼀引发争议的案例。
据了解，Muse 发布后不久，就有研究⼈员发现  Muse 曾存在可以让本地应⽤或终端命令控制
Agent 的严重漏洞；还有⼈发现，Muse 可以在特定提⽰下暴露其完整⽂件系统，包括  Ubuntu
系统⽂件、应⽤模板、内部⽂档以及明⽂存储的记忆⽂件。
对此，Meta 的回应是，Muse 运⾏在隔离的  Linux 虚拟机中，并称这并不意味着  Meta 基础设
施或其他⽤⼾数据遭到访问。
⽽  Robb 这次其实算是⽐较幸运的：因为他住在有安保的公寓⾥，买家也只是⽩跑了⼀趟。
如果类似事情发⽣在独居⼥性、没有⻔禁的⼩区，或者涉及更敏感的个⼈信息，⻛险可能完全
不同。正如⼀名⽹友所说：“ 幸好你不是独居⼥性，这太危险了。 ”这条评论获得了超过  600 个
赞，Robb ⾃⼰也承认这⼀点： “ 幸好我住的是有安保的公寓。 ” 
过去，⼤模型出现幻觉，最坏的情况可能是给你⼀段错误代码、⼀条不存在的资料。但  Agent
不⼀样——它拥有账号、浏览器、⽀付⽅式、联系⼈甚⾄现实世界⾥的⾏动⼊⼝，⼀旦  AI 的判
断出现偏差，错误就可能从屏幕⾥直接延伸到现实世界。
那么，你⽇常会使⽤  Agent 吗？以及你让  Agent 帮你完成任务时，是否也发⽣过类似事情？

## Document 390: “过去两年最准”的摩根大通交易台“翻多”

Source PDF: all report pdf/“过去两年最准”的摩根大通交易台“翻多”.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

其⽀撑逻辑涵盖宏观超预期、消费者韧性、盈利预期偏低、收益率趋稳及技术⾯改善五⼤
⽀柱。上次 8 ⽉ 31 ⽇转向后，多纳斯达克 100 、空罗素 2000 配对交易累计涨逾 8% ，此番 " 翻
多 " 说服⼒更强。策略上，科技仍为核⼼多头，但对冲⼯具从做空 RTY 转向衍⽣品，同时提
⽰⻓期加息⻛险犹存。
摘要：
摩根⼤通机构市场情报交易台（ Market Intel Desk ）以过去两年精准捕捉市场拐点著称，如今再
度发出重要信号 ——由 " 战术性谨慎 / 中性 " 转向 " 战术性看多 "。这⼀转变发⽣在宏观与微观数据
密集发布的关键窗⼝前，引发市场⼴泛关注。
最新报告显⽰，该交易台将⽴场转变的核⼼依据归结为五⼤⽀柱：宏观基本⾯超预期、消费者
韧性持续、盈利预期偏低、债券收益率趋于稳定，以及技术⾯改善。与此同时，交易台在策略
上调整了此前的多纳斯达克 100 （ NDX ）、空罗素 2000 （ RTY ）的配对交易，转⽽更倾向于以衍
⽣品⼯具表达观点。
值得注意的是，该交易台最近⼀次⽴场调整发⽣在 8 ⽉ 31 ⽇，彼时由看多转为 " 战术性谨慎 / 中

性 " ，此后标普 500 指数基本持平，⽽其推荐的多 NDX/ 空 RTY 配对交易累计上涨逾 8% ，美股整体
也跑赢其他主要市场。此次再度 " 翻多 " ，意味着该团队认为短期市场⻛险收益⽐已明显改善。
精准预判背书， " 翻多 " 信号分量⼗⾜
过去两年间，摩根⼤通机构市场情报交易台在多个关键节点的判断均被市场⾛势所验证，使其
在机构投资者群体中积累了较⾼的参考价值。
最近⼀次⽴场切换发⽣在 8 ⽉ 31 ⽇，该交易台将观点从看多下调⾄ " 战术性谨慎 / 中性 " ，理由涵盖
美联储政策不确定性、持仓结构、信⽤利差⾛阔、季节性负⾯效应，以及动量因⼦的回撤压
⼒。彼时，该团队建议将仓位集中于多 NDX 、空 RTY 的配对交易。
结果印证了这⼀判断：⾃该调整以来， SPX ⼏乎原地踏步，⽽多 NDX/ 空 RTY 配对交易累计涨幅
超过 8% ，美股整体表现也持续领先其他主要市场。正是这⼀背景，赋予了此次 " 翻多 " 信号更强
的市场说服⼒。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

五⼤理由⽀撑 " 翻多 " 逻辑
该交易台在最新报告中系统阐述了转向看多的五项核⼼依据：
第⼀，宏观基本⾯超预期。最新闪值 PMI 数据显⽰经济增⻓强于市场预期，有望⽀撑就业、消费
及企业盈利。该团队强调，标普 500 的每股收益（ EPS ）增⻓与名义 GDP 增速挂钩，⽽⾮实际
GDP ，当前经济⽔平意味着市场对盈利的预期可能过于保守。
第⼆，消费者韧性持续超预期。尽管市场对消费者在通胀与⾼利率环境下的承压能⼒存有疑
虑，但实际消费数据持续好于预期， "K 型复苏 " 假说受到挑战。与此同时，劳动⼒市场的改善正
进⼀步⽀撑消费端表现。
第三，盈利预期存在上修空间。2026 年第⼆季度 EPS 增速⾼达 52% ，市场⽬前预期第三季度约
为 29% ，该团队认为这⼀预测可能偏低 —— 标普 500 全部 11 个⾏业均预计实现正收⼊增⻓和正
EPS 增⻓。
第四，债券收益率或趋于稳定。摩根⼤通利率策略师 Jay Barry 预计， 10 年期美债收益率将在未
来⼀个⽉内从 5.18% ⼩幅升⾄ 5.20% ，随后于年底回落⾄ 5.05% 。收益率波动性的下降，将有助

于缓解股市所承受的部分下⾏压⼒。
第五，技术⾯持续改善。季节性因素转为有利，散⼾投资者参与度有望提升，整体持仓结构也
相对轻盈，为市场上⾏提供了空间。
短期催化剂密集，关注数据窗⼝
该交易台同时梳理了近期可能触发市场波动的关键催化剂：
通胀数据⽅⾯，本周将相继公布 PCE 和 ISM 制造业数据，其中 ISM 制造业价格⽀付分项是重要的
前瞻性通胀指标，服务业价格⽀付分项影响更为显著。若数据超预期偏热，可能导致收益率曲
线熊市平坦化。 CPI 数据将于 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇公布，美联储议息会议则于 10 ⽉ 28 ⽇召开。

### Page 3

劳动⼒市场⽅⾯，就业数据改善有助于强化增⻓叙事和消费逻辑，但在当前市场定价框架
下， " 好消息即坏消息 " 的逻辑可能再度主导 —— 强劲的就业数据或促使市场进⼀步定价加息预
期。
AI 主题⽅⾯， META 旗下 Muse 的发布为该主题注⼊新动能，本周 OpenAI 开发者⽇及美光科技
（ MU ）财报将成为重要观察窗⼝。
策略调整：科技仍是核⼼多头，配对交易转向衍⽣品
在具体操作层⾯，该交易台对持仓策略进⾏了明确调整：
科技板块仍是核⼼多头，但不再建议以做空 RTY 作为对冲，原因在于油价和收益率若进⼀步下
⾏，可能引发 RTY 的轧空⾏情。该团队建议转⽽通过衍⽣品⼯具来表达这⼀轧空⻛险的敞⼝。
科技板块内部，该团队预计将出现结构性扩散，半导体和 "Mag 7" 成分股有望跑赢科技板块整
体。软件板块虽尚未形成共识，但正在缓慢积累⼈⽓，被视为⾮共识性看多机会。 AI 主题的持续
性获得认可，该团队建议持续持有相关敞⼝。
科技板块以外，该团队认为，周期性板块若要出现持续性反弹，需要看到收益率曲线出现⽜市

陡峭化。在⾮ AI 相关领域，银⾏股因受益于经济重启、潜在的曲线陡峭化以及资本市场前景改善
⽽获得⻘睐。
对于利率敏感型板块 —— 房地产、电信和公⽤事业 —— 该团队认为，这些板块或可参与轧空⾏
情，但⼤概率将持续跑输标普 500 （ AI 相关标的除外）。
最⼤的潜在催化剂，该团队指出，若美伊达成协议，将推动欧洲股市和 RTY ⼤幅⾛⾼；此外，
新冠疫情后的复苏主题（航空、汽⻋、邮轮、可选消费、酒店等）同样值得关注，建议优先通
过期权⽅式布局。
⻓期⻛险犹存， " 翻多 " 并⾮⽆条件乐观
尽管短期⽴场转为看多，该交易台仍明确列出了若⼲尚未消散的中⻓期隐忧：
其⼀，债券市场是否低估了本轮美联储加息周期的终点？若经济按闪值 PMI 所⽰以 5% 的实际增
速扩张，且通胀压⼒持续上升，加之就业持续改善推⾼薪资通胀，则可能需要更为激进的加息
路径。
其⼆，市场是否会开始定价美联储在某次会议上加息 50 个基点？

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂

地產 中立 升息對REITs喜憂參半，轉注意現金流成長動能。

《升息不利地產REITs，但租賃期短者表現較佳》
利率變動會直接牽動房地產投資信託基金 (REITs)的
融資成本增加 ，壓縮可分配現金流與股利 ，依高盛

研究報告顯示，從2006年起的10年期美國國債收益
率上升至少40個基點的滾動六個月週期內，REITs的
表現比標普 500指數低 6.7%，過去五年 ，大多數
REIT 與 10 年期公債殖利率呈負相關，主要因為受
到租賃期間長短的影響 ，其中以飯店業及倉儲設施
因租賃期間較短可當下反應租金變動 ，購物中心因
經濟表現佳 ，可支配所得增加 ，所以表現反而較佳 ；
淨租賃、醫療保健REITs表現最差，但以近期而言，
業績 及營運模式的調整重要性也上升 ，醫療 保健
REITs受個別基本面不同，部分業者營運模式從過去
較長天期的淨租賃轉往養老住宅 ，縮短租賃期，老
年人口財富負擔能力提升，支撐老人住房市場定價

《預期地產交易價格降溫 ，4Q26交易量增長放緩 》

Green Street CPPI 數據顯示，全美商業地產價格
相較於低點已呈現微幅回升 ，8月上漲0.8%。過去
一年累計上漲5%，但美國國債收益率的上升及對借
貸成本的影響 ，恐促使購房者重新考慮支付意願 ，
預計漲勢即將降溫。依高盛統計截至8月的季度累計
地產交易量年增 18%(扣除合併 )，高於世邦魏理仕
和仲量聯行對 3Q26投資銷售額年增 13%和14%的
預期，8月份的強勁成長主要由工業地產和辦公地產
推動，而多戶住宅市場仍然落後 。工業倉儲主要受
惠供應過剩逐漸下降 ，租賃活動受到美國製造業回
流、近岸外包與第三方物流供應鏈重組支撐 ，優質
近港與門戶型物流中心空置率開始回落；辦公室物
業則呈現科技與專業服務企業偏好承租一線 A級商
辦，而不青睞 B/C級商辦 ，拆除舊型辦公室 、改建
住宅等 。多戶住宅仍在消化陽光帶 (德州、佛州等)
過剩的存量，壓抑短期租金漲幅 。受30年期固定房
貸利率在9月升至7%的高位及去年基期也高的影響 ，
預期4Q26交易量放緩。

《REITs股息吸引力低，轉注意現金流成長動能》
美REITs的回報在利率下降時會擴張估值，但現美國

英尺/日，YOY+23%，預估2H26與上半年量相同 ，
1H27再增加，預估美國4Q26天然氣價格將季節性
走升，持平或略低於去年。卡達能源LNG船已開始

但僅達 67%，遠低於過往 ，TTF價格大漲吸引運往

TTF價格將回落。

《成品油供應緊張，有利維持高煉油利潤》
受煉油廠關廠 ，煉油產能因俄烏 、中東戰事受損 ，
成品油供應比原油更緊張 ，美國汽、柴油庫存年比
減少，估計全球煉油廠開工率年減 700萬桶/日以上，

勁，美國煉油廠產能利用率已拉高到 98%高檔，雖
因4Q26汽油淡季使獲利季比下滑，但年比仍大幅成
長，其下行風險在於川普要求煉油商降價。
《4Q26能源類股較看好煉油廠》
預估美綜合油企 4Q26受惠油價高於預期 、LNG出
口增加，柴油高裂解價差 ，但進入汽油需求淡季 ，
獲利季比下滑 ，但年比仍大幅成長 ，現金流仍強大 ，
維持資本紀律及股東回報 。但在消息面上 ，受成品
油較原油供應吃緊，4Q26煉油商柴油價差仍佳，較
看好煉油商。

力、室內裝修與新成屋交屋塗裝 ，但因氣候影響戶

《4Q26美鋼價上漲；政策支撐4Q26鐵礦砂價》
七月美國鋼廠出貨量低於六月 ，但仍優於去年同期
5.4%，受鋼價調漲，今年美國進口鋼材量逐季增加 ，
進口市占率預估七月提高1%至18%，累積七月市占
率約16%，境內開工率約 81%，已在過去循環相對
高點。美對加拿大進口商品徵收的 50%關稅範圍 ，
部分新稅率疊加在原有的 232條款50%關稅之上 ，
推升美境內鋼價進一步走升，預期4Q26美鋼廠財報
將優於預期，但因淡季因素，獲利將低於3Q26。中
國重點城市推出放寬買房限制 、買房補貼和提高貸
款成數等政策 ，使中國百大房企今年前七月銷售同

比降幅較前六月收窄 ，今年上半年製造業 、基礎設
施投資同比也下降 ，八月發改委督促地方政府加速
重大計畫建設 ，將加快新增專項債發行速度 ，也推
出延長房貸年限至 40年，預期9月旺季來臨 ，鋼廠

《預期金價短期承壓，但中期仍看升》
近月金價歷經美國就業數據好壞參半 、美伊衝突情
勢升級，推升升息預期，美國計畫加大回購美國長
期國債規模 ，但低於市場預期 ，長期公債殖利率上
升，美元走弱等多空影響 ，本月金價在 4300-4600
美元間震盪 。目前美伊衝突由軍事增加經濟制裁 ，
川普認為期中選舉後可結束伊朗戰爭 ，變數仍多 ，
預期通膨放緩變慢 ，至明年才有較大回落 。但本行
研判應該不會升息 ，若美伊衝突帶來的不利因素漸
放緩，FED轉鴿派或市場縮減升息轉降息預期；受
擔憂債務可持續性 、分散交易對手風險及多元化資
產配置，中國人行加大購金步伐 ，據調查45%受訪
央行認為將未來一年內 將增加黃金儲備 ，年底可望
進入黃金需求旺季，維持金價中期上行。

要旨
 トランプ政権は保守主義を重視、共和党支持層から支持を受けるも、
物価問題で無党派層の支持を得られず、中間選挙に不安を抱える
 米国のイラン侵攻作戦はこう着、事態は長期化。一方、ウクライナで
は停戦仲介のタイミングを模索、ロシア、ウクライナ双方に圧力
 米国、中国は5月の首脳会談で建設的戦略安定関係の構築で合意。
お互いに時間を買う対応をとったといえ、米国はレアアースの供給網
構築、中国は経済立て直しが当面の課題
 米国は台湾へのコミットメント維持。中国も台湾統一は長期的な目標。
日米同盟が着実に深化する中、第一列島線の東側での中国の軍事
活動が当面の懸念要因

美联储内部分歧浮现，就业数据成关键变量
美联储内部信号并⾮⼀致。 Philip Jefferson 在准备好的讲话中提⽰不必急于⾏动，纽约联储主席
John Williams 此前也表⽰，在 9 ⽉联邦公开市场委员会会议决定加息之后，没有紧迫理由考虑再

度加息。
然⽽，达拉斯联储主席 Lorie Logan 持明显不同⽴场。她表⽰，要充分抑制通胀，美联储必须继
续加息，并估计联邦基⾦利率⽬标区间还需进⼀步上调⾄少 50 个基点，以平衡双重授权⽬标的
前景与⻛险。
这⼀分歧令市场对就业数据愈发敏感。据彭博调查经济学家预测， 9 ⽉⾮农就业⼈数预计新增约
9 万，低于前⽉的 16.2 万。但 BMO 资本市场美国利率策略主管 Ian Lyngen 指出，若数据显⽰劳动
⼒市场出现早期压⼒迹象，市场的反应可能不成⽐例地偏向债券上涨；⽽若数据符合预期甚⾄
略强，收益率则可能重拾升势。
T. Rowe Price 集团投资级负责⼈兼组合经理 Steve Boothe 表⽰，要使美债从就业数据中获得提
振，需要接近零甚⾄负值的就业增⻓，且薪资数据也需明显低于预期， " 劳动⼒市场成为反弹催
化剂的⻔槛实际上相当⾼。 "
不过，分析⼈⼠警告，本轮美债反弹更多反映技术性修复和短暂的避险情绪，⽽⾮基本⾯的根
本改变。
推动收益率持续⾛⾼的结构性因素依然存在：油价徘徊在每桶 100 美元上⽅，美国联邦政府巨额

⾚字⽀出与⼈⼯智能投资热潮为经济扩张持续加温，今年通胀同⽐已突破 3% ，⾼于美联储⽬标
区间。期货交易员虽已⼩幅下调加息押注规模，并将下⼀次加息时间预期推迟⾄ 12 ⽉会议，但
仍预计到 7 ⽉前⾄少还有三次 25 个基点的加息。
Federated Hermes 固定收益策略师兼组合经理 Karen Manna 表⽰，⾃ 9 ⽉ 16 ⽇美联储加息以
来，她对债市的悲观程度有所减弱，但尚未确信收益率已经⻅顶。 " 利率向⾼位推进的相当⼀部
分预期已经实现， " 她说， " 但仍可能继续⾛⾼。 "
“ 更温和的美国通胀数据、 10 ⽉加息预期降温以及油价回落为⾦价提供了⼀定喘息空间，但持续

## Document 734: 美联储6比1投票通过压力测试改革：资本要求波动性减半，唯一反对票警告韧性受损

Source PDF: all report pdf/美联储6比1投票通过压力测试改革：资本要求波动性减半，唯一反对票警告韧性受损.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美联储正式批准两项最终规则，旨在提升透明度、减少资本要求波动性约 50% 。新规将公
开征求压⼒测试情景意⻅，并对连续两年测试结果取均值计算资本缓冲，均值机制 2028 年
⽣效。此次改⾰是银⾏业多年博弈成果，⾏业团体对此表⽰欢迎。然⽽，理事 Barr 等批评
者警告，改⾰将削弱压⼒测试约束⼒，降低银⾏体系整体韧性。
摘要：
美联储以 6 ⽐ 1 的投票结果，正式通过对年度银⾏压⼒测试的重⼤改⾰，为华尔街⼤型银⾏的多
年博弈画上句号。
9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，美联储正式批准了两项最终规则，旨在提升压⼒测试的透明度与公众监督⼒度，并减
少相关资本要求的波动性。这两项规则与 2025 年发布的征求意⻅稿基本保持⼀致。
根据新规，美联储将公开征求对压⼒测试情景及重⼤模型变更的意⻅，并更新假设情景框架、
测试⽇历及全球市场冲击组成部分。美联储表⽰，相关变化预计将使资本要求的年度波动性降
低约 50% ，且不会对整体资本要求产⽣实质性影响。
唯⼀投票反对的美联储理事 Michael Barr 警告称，此次改⾰将 " 显著削弱压⼒测试，进⽽削弱银

⾏的抗⻛险能⼒ " 。前美联储官员、智库 Better Markets 银⾏政策总监 Christopher Appel 亦对改
⾰的实际效果持怀疑态度。
美联储理事会定期开展压⼒测试，旨在保障⼤型银⾏具备充⾜的资本，确保即使在严重经济衰
退期间，依然能够稳健地向家庭和企业发放贷款。此前于 2024 年 12 ⽉，理事会已宣布将优化压
⼒测试的⽅法体系，以进⼀步增强⾦融体系的抗⻛险能⼒。
核⼼变化：透明度提升，双年均值计算起步
美联储监管副主席 Michelle Bowman 在声明中表⽰，此次改⾰ " 通过确保压⼒测试的透明性、颗
粒度和⻛险敏感性，维护了其韧性 " ，公众将获得更充分的保证，确保银⾏所承担的⻛险能够在
压⼒损失和资本要求中得到合理反映。
两项最终规则的内容与 2025 年提案基本⼀致。
其⼀，美联储将每年邀请公众对压⼒测试情景及重⼤模型变更提交意⻅，并同步更新假设情景
设计框架、调整测试⽇历，以及更新适⽤于⼤型交易账簿银⾏的全球市场冲击组成部分。相关
银⾏每年将接受两套全球市场冲击测试，美联储将以产⽣最⼤损失的情景计算测试结果。

其⼆，在计算压⼒资本缓冲要求时，美联储将对连续两年参与压⼒测试的机构取最近两次年度
测试结果的均值。为确保计算中仅使⽤纳⼊公众意⻅的模型，该平均机制将于 2028 年起正式⽣
效。
美联储同步宣布，就改进⾮利息收⼊模型征求公众意⻅，旨在更好地捕捉不同银⾏商业模式在
创收⽅式上的差异，意⻅征集期为在《联邦公报》刊登后 60 天内。
⾏业博弈：诉讼推动改⾰提速
此次改⾰是银⾏业多年施压的成果。

压⼒测试制度建⽴于 2008 年⾦融危机之后，旨在评估⼤型银⾏在假设经济衰退情景下的资本充
⾜性。
美联储于 2024 年 12 ⽉宣布将对测试进⾏修订，但当⽉⾏业团体代表摩根⼤通、⾼盛等机构，随
即对美联储提起诉讼，要求在规则制定过程中获得更多透明度和参与权。

等机构对新规表⽰欢迎，并在联合声明中称：
反对声⾳：测试或沦为 " 空洞仪式 "
尽管改⾰获得⼴泛⽀持，批评者仍对其⽅向提出质疑。
美联储理事 Michael Barr认为，此次改⾰将实质性削弱压⼒测试的约束⼒，从⽽降低银⾏体系
的整体韧性。

益空洞的练习 " 来设定资本要求，⽽应将压⼒测试作为⼀种灵活的监管⼯具，⽤于发现脆弱性、
挑战银⾏假设并推动纠正⾏动。
今年早些时候，⼤型银⾏在年度压⼒测试中再度全部通过，随后⽴即向股东⼤规模返还资本，
通过股息和股票回购发放的⾦额打破了 2019 年创下的历史纪录。

透明度和公众参与造就了更完善的压⼒测试框架，有助于提⾼准确性，使受监管银⾏得以开展更
审慎的资本规划，并为国家经济带来积极效益。

## Document 764: 英中銀、11月利上げに傾く ～ＱＴは国債市場の安定を優先～

Source PDF: all report pdf/英中銀、11月利上げに傾く ～ＱＴは国債市場の安定を優先～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-09-18
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年９月 18 日

1/3 
英中銀、11 月利上げに傾く 
～ＱＴは国債市場の安定を優先～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ 英イングランド銀行は９月の金融政策委員会で政策金利を3.75％に据え置いた。 投票結果は
前回と同じ６対３だったが、中東情勢の長期化とエネルギー価格の上昇を受け、インフレへ
の警戒を一段と強めた。据え置きを支持した６名のうち、ベイリー総裁を含む４名も利上げ
に前向きな姿勢を強めており、11 月会合で利上げに踏み切る可能性が高まっている。 
○ 一方、長期金利の上昇や国債市場の安定性に配慮し、ＱＴのペースを大幅に減速するととも
に、市場での国債売却を当面停止し、来年４月までに政府の債務管理に統合する新たな売却
方針を検討する。

■利上げ見送りもインフレ警戒を強める 
英イングランド銀行（ＢＯＥ）は９月の金融政策委員会（ＭＰＣ）で、前回７月の会合と同様
に６対３の賛成多数で政策金利を3.75％に据え置いた。前回会合以降、中東情勢の緊張が一段と
高まっており、原油や天然ガス価格は７月の金融政策レポート（旧物価レポート）の想定を大き
く上回っている。17 日に発表された８月の消費者物価は、エネルギー価格の上昇加速を受け、前
年比＋3.1％と５ヶ月振りに３％を上回った。ＢＯＥによれば、足元のエネルギー価格を機械的に
反映しただけでも、消費者物価は来年１～３月期に４％をやや上回る可能性があり、これは７月
時点の見通しを大きく上回る。 
声明文では、 「物価見通しに対するリスクは上振れ方向に傾いており、7 月の金融政策レポート
の時点よりも更にその傾向が強まっている」と、インフレへの警戒姿勢を強めている。それにも
かかわらず今回利上げを見送った背景には、エネルギー高による企業の販売価格や賃金への二次
的波及がまだほとんど確認されていないこと、労働市場に依然としてスラック（緩み）が残って
いること、金融環境が既に相当引き締まっていることがある。 
ただ、前回会合以降に発表された景気指標は、４～６月期の実質国内総生産（ＧＤＰ）が前期
比＋0.4％とＢＯＥの予想（同＋0.3％）を上回り、７月の月次ＧＤＰも前月比＋0.4％と堅調な景
気拡大が続いている。雇用指標にも安定化の兆しが広がっており、ＢＯＥも労働市場のスラック
が拡大を続けるとの従来の見方を改め、安定化しつつあると判断している。景気や労働市場の弱

### Page 2

2/3 
さを理由に利上げを見送る根拠は徐々に薄れつつある。

■据え置き派もタカ派姿勢を強める 
ベイリー総裁を含む６人のメンバーは、前回７月の会合と同様に据え置きに投票したが、議事
要旨に記された個別意見をみると、タカ派への傾斜が進んでいる。ベイリー総裁は、エネルギー
や食料価格の上振れリスクを強調したうえで、中東紛争の長期化によって二次的波及リスクが高
まれば、金融政策を引き締める必要が生じる可能性が高いとの立場を示した。ロンバルデリ副総
裁も、インフレリスクが前回よりも更に上方にシフトしたことを認め、紛争が長引くほど利上げ
の根拠が強まると指摘している。ラムスデン副総裁も、国内のインフレ圧力はなお比較的穏やか
であるとしながらも、労働市場のスラックが底打ちしつつある兆候や景気が予想以上に底堅いこ
とから、物価の上振れ圧力が続けば利上げが必要になると論じている。ブリーデン副総裁も、エ
ネルギーショックの規模と持続期間が拡大するなか、二次的波及の可能性が高まったとし、リス
クが顕在化すれば政策金利の引き上げで対応することが一段と適切になるとの認識を示した。ハ
ト派姿勢を継続するディングラ委員とテイラー委員の２名を除く据え置き派の４名は、利上げ支
持に傾いている。 
前回同様に25bpの利上げを主張した３名も、利上げへの確信を強めている。グリーン委員は、
景気や雇用指標の底堅さから労働市場のスラックが既にピークを打った可能性があると指摘し、
二次的波及の決定的な証拠を待てば、政策が後手に回ると警告を発している。マン委員は、2027
年初めにかけて消費者物価が一段と加速し、そのタイミングが春の賃金交渉と重なることを警戒
しており、利上げが必要と主張している。ピル委員（チーフ・エコノミスト）は、エネルギーシ
ョックが７月時点の想定よりも長期化し、景気が予想以上に底堅いことから、利上げによって物
価安定へのコミットメントを明確に示すべきと主張している。 
投票結果は７月と同じ６対３だったが、据え置きを主張した委員の多くが利上げに傾いてお
り、ＭＰＣ全体の政策姿勢は明確にタカ派方向にシフトした。中東情勢の緊張とエネルギー価格
の高止まりが続けば、次回11 月のＭＰＣで利上げに踏み切る可能性が高まる。11 月には新たな金
融政策レポートも公表されるため、エネルギー高の持続性や二次的波及の兆候を点検したうえ
で、利上げの是非を判断することになるだろう。

■国債市場に配慮しＱＴを大幅減速 
なお、ＢＯＥは今回のＭＰＣで、量的引き締め（ＱＴ）の方針を大きく変更した。過去１年
（2025 年10 月～2026 年９月）は、満期償還と市場売却を通じて保有国債を年間で700 億ポンド
削減し、長期金利の上昇圧力を抑えるため、市場売却分については長期債を少なめにする「短期
40％・中期40％・長期20％」の構成としてきた。今回は毎年９月に向こう１年間のＱＴの削減方
針を決める方式を改め、2034 年までの複数年の計画を公表した。新たな計画では、資産買い入れ
ファシリティ（ＡＰＦ）を通じて保有する国債4880 億ポンドのうち、最も年限の長い1200 億ポ
ンドをＱＴの対象から除外し、残りの3680 億ポンドの保有国債については、2034 年までに満期を

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
迎える 2220 億ポンドを原則として満期まで保有し、残りの1460 億ポンドを年200 億ポンドのペ
ースで売却する。これにより、満期償還分を含めた保有国債の削減ペースは、2034 年までの年平
均で 460 億ポンドと、過去１年間の 700 億ポンドから大幅に減速する。 
国債の売却方法も大きく変更する。ＢＯＥはこれまで市場参加者を対象とするオークションを
通じて保有国債を直接売却してきたが、こうした市場売却を当面停止する。そのうえで、2035～
49 年に満期を迎える1460 億ポンドについて、ＢＯＥが政府に市場価格で売却し、財務省の指示を
受けた債務管理庁（ＤＭＯ）が買い取る新たな方式を検討する。2027 年４月までに最終判断し、
導入する場合には、ＤＭＯの年間資金調達計画に組み込む形で実施を開始する。それまではＢＯ
Ｅによる市場向けの国債売却オークションを停止する。ＱＴの削減ペースを落とすだけでなく、
市場への直接売却を停止し、超長期債1200 億ポンドをＱＴの対象から切り離すことで、国債市場
の需給や市場機能への負荷を抑える狙いがある。このように、今回の変更では、長期・超長期国
債を中心とする金利の上昇圧力と国債市場の安定確保に配慮した。政策金利ではインフレ再燃へ
の警戒を強める一方、ＱＴでは市場安定に配慮するという、異なる方向の政策対応を打ち出した
点も今回のＭＰＣの特徴と言えよう。

以 上

## Document 820: 高盛策略师警示：盈利支撑暂稳股市，美债抛售若提速，美股或将承压

Source PDF: all report pdf/高盛策略师警示：盈利支撑暂稳股市，美债抛售若提速，美股或将承压.pdf

Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛策略师 Peter Oppenheimer 认为，健康的企业盈利仍能部分抵消⾼收益率对美股估值
的压⼒，名义经济扩张也为企业营收提供⽀撑。但若债券收益率快速上⾏，尤其是调整速
度加快，这⼀缓冲将明显减弱，股市可能迅速承压。
摘要：
全球债券收益率徘徊在数⼗年⾼位附近，美股韧性正⾯临考验。
⾼盛⾸席全球股票策略师 Peter Oppenheimer 警告称，如果债券市场抛售进⼀步加剧，尤其是收
益率以较快速度上⾏，美国股市可能迅速变得脆弱。他表⽰，真正需要关注的是债券市场是否
会从当前⽔平出现更⼤幅度、更加剧烈的抛售。他称： “ 在这种情况下，我认为股市将变得脆
弱。 ”
他指出，今年企业盈利表现较为健康，在⼀定程度上消化了美股估值压⼒，为股市抵御债券收
益率上⾏提供了⼀定缓冲。
⽬前，股市尚未因债券市场持续抛售⽽受到明显冲击。但随着全球平均债券收益率在这个动荡
的 9 ⽉结束之际仍处于数⼗年⾼位附近，债券收益率上⾏与企业盈利增⻓之间的拉锯，正成为市
场关注的焦点。
盈利仍提供⽀撑，但缓冲并⾮⽆限

Oppenheimer 认为，当前美国股票市场的盈利基本⾯依然较为积极。经过数年的利润增⻓，企
业仍受益于名义经济扩张，通胀和实际产出增⻓共同推⾼企业营收，为股市提供了⼀定⽀撑。
但这种⽀撑并⾮没有边界。他强调，如果债券收益率进⼀步⾛⾼，尤其是上升速度过快，股市
仍将⾯临明显压⼒。“ 我们过去发现，关键不仅在于收益率的绝对⽔平，还在于调整的速度，以
及推动收益率变化的原因。 ”他说。
这意味着，⾼收益率本⾝并不必然触发股市调整，更值得警惕的是收益率在短期内快速上⾏。
只要盈利增⻓仍能消化部分估值压⼒，股市就可能维持韧性；但如果⽆⻛险利率快速抬升，估
值承压的速度也会随之加快。
市场分歧聚焦收益率还能涨多快
本轮债券抛售背后，市场对能源价格上涨可能推⾼通胀、进⽽影响美联储利率路径的担忧有所
升温。周三，美债收益率⼀度暂缓此前的上⾏势头，此前公布的美国经济数据释放出⼀定的降
温迹象，但全球债券收益率整体仍处于数⼗年⾼位附近。
围绕收益率进⼀步上⾏对股市的影响，华尔街机构也存在分歧。摩根⼠丹利的 Marina Zavolock

认为，即便美债市场出现新⼀轮抛售，股市仍可能保持⼀定韧性。
巴克莱策略师 Emmanuel Cau 领衔的团队则更为谨慎。据报道，该团队认为，随着利率上升削弱
“ 别⽆选择 ” （ TINA ）交易的吸引⼒，股市上涨动能正在减弱。
市场当前⾯临的核⼼⽭盾因此并⾮单纯的 “ ⾼收益率 ” 与 “ ⾼估值 ” 之争，⽽是企业盈利增⻓能否
继续抵消利率上⾏带来的估值压⼒。如果收益率缓慢上⾏，盈利仍可能为股市提供缓冲；但若
债券抛售加速，利率快速攀升，股市⾯临的压⼒可能明显放⼤。
